package app.jacket.mobile.data

import org.jsoup.Jsoup
import org.jsoup.nodes.Element
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URI
import java.net.URL
import java.net.URLEncoder
import java.nio.charset.Charset
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.Callable

/**
 * In-app HTML tracker adapter.
 *
 * It parses result markup and direct links published by the tracker. It does not
 * solve CAPTCHA, bypass anti-bot challenges, or automate account verification.
 */
internal data class TrackerSearchBatchOutcome(
    val results: List<SourceResult>,
    val failures: List<String>,
    val completedProviders: Int = 0,
    val totalProviders: Int = 0,
    val timedOut: Boolean = false
)

object TrackerSearchClient {
    private const val MAX_HTML_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
    private const val MAX_RESULTS = 40
    private const val MAX_DETAIL_PAGES = 10
    private const val MAX_DETAIL_CONCURRENCY = 4
    private const val SEARCH_ALL_TIMEOUT_MS = 25_000L
    private const val DETAIL_RESOLUTION_TIMEOUT_MS = 12_000L
    private const val DETAIL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS = 3_000
    private const val DETAIL_READ_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000
    private const val CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000
    private const val READ_TIMEOUT_MS = 12_000
    private const val USER_AGENT = "JacketAndroid/2.6 (in-app tracker search)"
    private val REDIRECT_CODES = setOf(301, 302, 303, 307, 308)

    private data class Profile(
        val charset: Charset,
        val detailSelectors: List<String>
    )

    internal data class Candidate(
        val title: String,
        val pageUrl: String?,
        val magnet: String?,
        val torrentUrl: String?,
        val rowText: String,
        val sizeBytes: Long?,
        val seeders: Int?,
        val leechers: Int?
    )

    private data class HtmlPage(val finalUrl: String, val html: String)

    private val profiles = mapOf(
        "tracker-rutracker" to Profile(
            Charset.forName("windows-1251"),
            listOf("a.tLink", "a[href*='viewtopic.php']")
        ),
        "tracker-nnmclub" to Profile(
            Charset.forName("windows-1251"),
            listOf("a[href*='viewtopic.php']", "a.tLink")
        ),
        "tracker-rutor" to Profile(
            Charsets.UTF_8,
            listOf("a.detLink", "a[href*='/torrent/']")
        ),
        "tracker-kinozal" to Profile(
            Charset.forName("windows-1251"),
            listOf("a[href*='details.php?id=']")
        ),
        "tracker-1337x" to Profile(
            Charsets.UTF_8,
            listOf("a[href*='/torrent/']")
        ),
        "tracker-tpb" to Profile(
            Charsets.UTF_8,
            listOf("a[href*='torrent/']")
        )
    )

    fun supports(providerId: String): Boolean = providerId in profiles

    /** Encodes Cyrillic search text using the selected tracker's expected charset. */
    internal fun buildSearchUrl(provider: WebSearchProvider, query: String): String {
        val profile = profiles[provider.id]
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Для этого сайта нет встроенного парсера")
        require(query.isNotBlank()) { "Введите название для поиска" }
        val template = WebSearchUrlBuilder.validateTemplate(provider.searchUrlTemplate)
        val encoded = URLEncoder.encode(query.trim(), profile.charset.name()).replace("+", "%20")
        val result = template.replace("{query}", encoded)
        require(result.length <= 4_096) { "Ссылка поиска слишком длинная" }
        return result
    }

    /**
     * Searches up to six enabled built-in trackers concurrently, with a strict
     * per-provider concurrency cap. Partial results are retained if one site fails.
     */
    private data class ProviderSearchOutcome(
        val provider: WebSearchProvider,
        val results: List<SourceResult> = emptyList(),
        val error: String? = null
    )

    /**
     * Searches enabled trackers concurrently and publishes a merged snapshot each
     * time one finishes. Results are collected in completion order, not provider
     * settings order, and the whole batch has a hard time budget.
     */
    internal fun searchAll(
        providers: List<WebSearchProvider>,
        query: String,
        onUpdate: (TrackerSearchBatchOutcome) -> Unit = {}
    ): TrackerSearchBatchOutcome {
        require(query.isNotBlank()) { "Введите название для поиска" }
        val active = providers
            .filter { it.enabled && supports(it.id) }
            .distinctBy { it.id }
            .take(6)
        require(active.isNotEmpty()) { "Нет включённых встроенных трекеров" }

        val tasks = active.map { provider ->
            Callable {
                try {
                    ProviderSearchOutcome(provider, search(provider, query.trim()))
                } catch (error: Exception) {
                    val cause = error.cause ?: error
                    ProviderSearchOutcome(
                        provider = provider,
                        error = cause.message?.take(160) ?: cause.javaClass.simpleName
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        val batches = ArrayList<List<SourceResult>>(active.size)
        val failures = ArrayList<String>()
        val completedIds = HashSet<String>()
        var completedProviders = 0

        fun snapshot(timedOut: Boolean = false) = TrackerSearchBatchOutcome(
            results = mergeResults(batches),
            failures = failures.toList(),
            completedProviders = completedProviders,
            totalProviders = active.size,
            timedOut = timedOut
        )

        val completed = CompletionOrderCollector.collect(
            tasks = tasks,
            timeoutMs = SEARCH_ALL_TIMEOUT_MS,
            maxConcurrency = 3
        ) { result ->
            completedProviders++
            result.fold(
                onSuccess = { outcome ->
                    completedIds += outcome.provider.id
                    if (outcome.error != null) {
                        failures += "${outcome.provider.name}: ${outcome.error}"
                    } else {
                        batches += outcome.results
                    }
                },
                onFailure = { error ->
                    failures += "Ошибка обработчика трекера: ${error.message?.take(160) ?: error.javaClass.simpleName}"
                }
            )
            runCatching { onUpdate(snapshot()) }
        }

        val timedOut = completed < active.size
        if (timedOut) {
            active.filterNot { it.id in completedIds }.forEach { provider ->
                failures += "${provider.name}: превышено время поиска (25 секунд)"
            }
        }
        return snapshot(timedOut).also { outcome ->
            runCatching { onUpdate(outcome) }
        }
    }

    /** Merges provider batches by BitTorrent info-hash and keeps the healthiest duplicate. */
    internal fun mergeResults(batches: List<List<SourceResult>>): List<SourceResult> {
        val merged = LinkedHashMap<String, SourceResult>()
        batches.flatten().forEach { source ->
            val key = magnetInfoHash(source.magnetUri)
                ?: source.downloadUrl
                ?: source.id
            val current = merged[key]
            if (current == null || (source.seeders ?: -1) > (current.seeders ?: -1)) {
                merged[key] = source
            }
        }
        return merged.values
            .sortedWith(
                compareByDescending<SourceResult> { it.seeders ?: -1 }
                    .thenByDescending { it.sizeBytes ?: -1L }
                    .thenBy { it.title.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }
            )
            .take(MAX_RESULTS * 6)
    }

    fun search(provider: WebSearchProvider, query: String): List<SourceResult> {
        val profile = profiles[provider.id]
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Для этого сайта нет встроенного парсера")
        require(provider.enabled) { "Трекер отключён" }
        require(query.isNotBlank()) { "Введите название для поиска" }

        val searchUrl = buildSearchUrl(provider, query)
        val originalHost = URI(searchUrl).host
        val searchPage = fetchHtml(searchUrl, provider.cookie, originalHost, profile.charset)
        ensureNotChallenge(searchPage.html, provider.name)
        val candidates = parseListing(searchPage.html, searchPage.finalUrl, provider.id)
        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            throw IOException(
                "${provider.name}: не удалось распознать результаты. Возможно, сайт изменил разметку " +
                    "или требует вход через браузер."
            )
        }

        val detailRequests = candidates.withIndex()
            .filter { (_, item) ->
                item.magnet == null && item.torrentUrl == null && item.pageUrl != null
            }
            .take(MAX_DETAIL_PAGES)
            .map { it.index to it.value.pageUrl!! }

        val details = resolveDetailsConcurrently(
            detailRequests, provider, originalHost, profile.charset
        )

        val resolved = candidates.mapIndexedNotNull { index, candidate ->
            val detailLinks = details[index]
            val magnet = candidate.magnet ?: detailLinks?.first
            val torrent = candidate.torrentUrl ?: detailLinks?.second
            if (magnet == null && torrent == null) return@mapIndexedNotNull null

            val identity = magnetInfoHash(magnet) ?: torrent ?: candidate.pageUrl ?: candidate.title
            SourceResult(
                id = "${provider.id}:${identity.lowercase(Locale.ROOT).hashCode()}",
                title = candidate.title,
                providerName = "Встроенный парсер",
                magnetUri = magnet,
                downloadUrl = torrent,
                sizeBytes = candidate.sizeBytes,
                seeders = candidate.seeders,
                leechers = candidate.leechers,
                pageUrl = candidate.pageUrl,
                indexerId = provider.id,
                tracker = provider.name
            )
        }

        return resolved
            .distinctBy { magnetInfoHash(it.magnetUri) ?: it.downloadUrl ?: it.id }
            .sortedWith(
                compareByDescending<SourceResult> { it.seeders ?: -1 }
                    .thenBy { it.title.lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }
            )
            .take(MAX_RESULTS)
    }

    private fun resolveDetailsConcurrently(
        pages: List<Pair<Int, String>>,
        provider: WebSearchProvider,
        originalHost: String,
        fallbackCharset: Charset
    ): Map<Int, Pair<String?, String?>> {
        if (pages.isEmpty()) return emptyMap()
        val tasks = pages.map { (index, url) ->
            Callable {
                val page = fetchHtml(
                    url,
                    provider.cookie,
                    originalHost,
                    fallbackCharset,
                    connectTimeoutMs = DETAIL_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS,
                    readTimeoutMs = DETAIL_READ_TIMEOUT_MS
                )
                ensureNotChallenge(page.html, provider.name)
                index to parseDownloadLinks(page.html, page.finalUrl)
            }
        }
        val details = LinkedHashMap<Int, Pair<String?, String?>>()
        CompletionOrderCollector.collect(
            tasks = tasks,
            timeoutMs = DETAIL_RESOLUTION_TIMEOUT_MS,
            maxConcurrency = MAX_DETAIL_CONCURRENCY
        ) { result ->
            result.getOrNull()?.let { (index, links) -> details[index] = links }
        }
        return details
    }

    internal fun parseListing(html: String, baseUrl: String, providerId: String): List<Candidate> {
        val profile = profiles[providerId]
            ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Для этого сайта нет встроенного парсера")
        val document = Jsoup.parse(html, baseUrl)
        val candidates = LinkedHashMap<String, Candidate>()

        fun addCandidate(
            row: Element,
            title: String,
            pageUrl: String?,
            magnet: String?,
            torrentUrl: String?
        ) {
            val cleanTitle = title.replace(Regex("\\s+"), " ").trim().take(240)
            if (cleanTitle.isBlank()) return
            val identity = magnetInfoHash(magnet) ?: torrentUrl ?: pageUrl ?: cleanTitle
            val stats = parseStats(row)
            candidates.putIfAbsent(
                identity,
                Candidate(
                    cleanTitle,
                    pageUrl,
                    magnet,
                    torrentUrl,
                    row.text(),
                    stats.first,
                    stats.second,
                    stats.third
                )
            )
        }

        document.select("a[href^=magnet:]").forEach { anchor ->
            val row = resultContainer(anchor)
            val magnet = anchor.attr("href").takeIf { it.startsWith("magnet:", true) }
            addCandidate(row, titleFrom(row, anchor), firstDetailUrl(row, baseUrl), magnet, null)
        }

        document.select("a[href]").forEach { anchor ->
            val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@forEach
            val path = runCatching { URI(href).rawPath.orEmpty().lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }.getOrDefault("")
            if (!path.endsWith(".torrent")) return@forEach
            val row = resultContainer(anchor)
            addCandidate(row, titleFrom(row, anchor), null, null, href)
        }

        val detailSelector = profile.detailSelectors.joinToString(",")
        document.select(detailSelector).forEach { anchor ->
            val title = anchor.text().trim().ifBlank { return@forEach }
            val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@forEach
            if (href.startsWith("magnet:", true)) return@forEach
            val row = resultContainer(anchor)
            val rowMagnet = row.selectFirst("a[href^=magnet:]")?.attr("href")
                ?.takeIf { it.startsWith("magnet:", true) }
            val rowHash = magnetInfoHash(rowMagnet)
            if (rowHash != null && candidates.containsKey(rowHash)) return@forEach
            addCandidate(row, title, href, null, findTorrentLink(row, baseUrl))
        }

        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            document.select("tr, article, li").take(200).forEach { row ->
                val anchor = row.selectFirst("a[href]") ?: return@forEach
                val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@forEach
                if (!isDetailLink(href)) return@forEach
                val title = anchor.text().trim()
                if (title.length < 3) return@forEach
                addCandidate(row, title, href, null, findTorrentLink(row, baseUrl))
            }
        }
        return candidates.values.take(MAX_RESULTS)
    }

    internal fun parseDownloadLinks(html: String, baseUrl: String): Pair<String?, String?> {
        val document = Jsoup.parse(html, baseUrl)
        val magnet = document.selectFirst("a[href^=magnet:]")?.attr("href")
            ?.takeIf { it.startsWith("magnet:", true) }
        val torrent = document.select("a[href]").firstNotNullOfOrNull { anchor ->
            val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@firstNotNullOfOrNull null
            val path = runCatching { URI(href).rawPath.orEmpty().lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }.getOrDefault("")
            val label = anchor.text().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
            href.takeIf {
                path.endsWith(".torrent") ||
                    path.contains("download.php") ||
                    path.endsWith("/dl.php") ||
                    (label.contains("torrent") && (label.contains("скач") || label.contains("download")))
            }
        }
        return magnet to torrent
    }

    /** Built-in tracker requests must stay on public HTTPS hosts, including after redirects. */
    internal fun isSafeTrackerRequestUrl(urlValue: String): Boolean {
        val uri = runCatching { URI(urlValue.trim()) }.getOrNull() ?: return false
        val host = uri.host?.removePrefix("[")?.removeSuffix("]")?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
            ?: return false
        if (!uri.scheme.equals("https", ignoreCase = true) ||
            uri.rawUserInfo != null || uri.rawFragment != null
        ) return false

        if (host.matches(Regex("[0-9.]+"))) {
            val parts = host.split('.')
            if (parts.size != 4 || parts.any { part ->
                    part.isEmpty() || part.length > 3 || !part.all(Char::isDigit) ||
                        (part.length > 1 && part[0] == '0')
                }
            ) return false
            val octets = parts.map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: return false }
            if (octets.any { it !in 0..255 }) return false
            val a = octets[0]
            val b = octets[1]
            val c = octets[2]
            val nonPublic = a == 0 || a == 10 || a == 127 || a >= 224 ||
                (a == 169 && b == 254) ||
                (a == 172 && b in 16..31) ||
                (a == 192 && (b == 168 || (b == 0 && c in 0..2) || (b == 88 && c == 99))) ||
                (a == 198 && (b in 18..19 || (b == 51 && c == 100))) ||
                (a == 203 && b == 0 && c == 113) ||
                (a == 100 && b in 64..127)
            if (nonPublic) return false
        } else if (!host.contains('.') && !host.contains(':')) {
            return false
        }

        if (host.contains(':') && (
                host == "::" || host == "::1" ||
                host.startsWith("::ffff:") ||
                host.startsWith("fe8", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("fe9", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("fea", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("feb", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("fc", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("fd", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("2001:db8", ignoreCase = true) ||
                host.startsWith("ff", ignoreCase = true)
            )) {
            return false
        }
        val privateSuffixes = listOf(".internal", ".intranet", ".private", ".corp")
        return !LocalNetwork.isLocalHost(host) && privateSuffixes.none(host::endsWith)
    }


    private fun fetchHtml(
        urlValue: String,
        cookie: String,
        originalHost: String,
        fallbackCharset: Charset,
        connectTimeoutMs: Int = CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS,
        readTimeoutMs: Int = READ_TIMEOUT_MS
    ): HtmlPage {
        var current = urlValue
        for (redirect in 0..4) {
            if (!isSafeTrackerRequestUrl(current)) throw IOException("Трекер перенаправил запрос на небезопасный адрес")
            val uri = URI(current)
            val connection = (URL(current).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                requestMethod = "GET"
                connectTimeout = connectTimeoutMs
                readTimeout = readTimeoutMs
                instanceFollowRedirects = false
                setRequestProperty("User-Agent", USER_AGENT)
                setRequestProperty("Accept", "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8")
                setRequestProperty("Accept-Language", "ru-RU,ru;q=0.9,en;q=0.7")
                setRequestProperty("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
                if (cookie.isNotBlank() && uri.host.equals(originalHost, true)) {
                    setRequestProperty("Cookie", cookie.take(8_192))
                }
            }
            try {
                val status = connection.responseCode
                if (status in REDIRECT_CODES) {
                    if (redirect == 4) throw IOException("Слишком много перенаправлений трекера")
                    val location = connection.getHeaderField("Location")
                        ?: throw IOException("Трекер вернул перенаправление без Location")
                    current = URL(URL(current), location).toString()
                    continue
                }
                if (status == 401 || status == 403 || status == 429) {
                    throw IOException("HTTP $status от трекера. Проверьте Cookie или доступность сайта.")
                }
                if (status !in 200..299) throw IOException("HTTP $status от трекера")
                val bytes = connection.inputStream.use { readLimited(it, MAX_HTML_BYTES) }
                val html = decodeHtml(bytes, connection.getHeaderField("Content-Type"), fallbackCharset)
                return HtmlPage(connection.url.toString(), html)
            } finally {
                connection.disconnect()
            }
        }
        throw IOException("Не удалось получить страницу трекера")
    }

    private fun decodeHtml(bytes: ByteArray, contentType: String?, fallbackCharset: Charset): String {
        val headerCharset = Regex("(?i)charset\\s*=\\s*[\\\"']?([^;\\s\\\"']+)")
            .find(contentType.orEmpty())?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)
        val prefix = bytes.copyOfRange(0, minOf(bytes.size, 8_192)).toString(Charsets.ISO_8859_1)
        val metaCharset = Regex("(?is)<meta[^>]+charset=['\\\"]?([a-z0-9._-]+)")
            .find(prefix)?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)
        val charset = sequenceOf(headerCharset, metaCharset)
            .filterNotNull()
            .mapNotNull { name -> runCatching { Charset.forName(name) }.getOrNull() }
            .firstOrNull() ?: fallbackCharset
        return String(bytes, charset)
    }

    private fun readLimited(input: java.io.InputStream, maxBytes: Int): ByteArray {
        val output = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val buffer = ByteArray(8_192)
        var total = 0
        while (true) {
            val count = input.read(buffer)
            if (count < 0) break
            total += count
            if (total > maxBytes) throw IOException("Ответ трекера превышает допустимый размер")
            output.write(buffer, 0, count)
        }
        return output.toByteArray()
    }

    internal fun ensureNotChallenge(html: String, trackerName: String) {
        val document = Jsoup.parse(html)
        val title = document.title().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        val explicitChallenge =
            html.contains("cf-chl-", true) ||
                html.contains("challenge-platform", true) ||
                document.selectFirst(
                    "form#challenge-form, form[action*=captcha], iframe[src*=captcha], " +
                        ".g-recaptcha, .h-captcha, #captcha, .captcha"
                ) != null
        if (title.contains("just a moment") || explicitChallenge) {
            throw IOException(
                "$trackerName требует браузерную проверку или CAPTCHA. Пройдите её в обычном браузере " +
                    "и добавьте Cookie-сессию; обход защиты не выполняется."
            )
        }
    }

    private fun resultContainer(anchor: Element): Element =
        anchor.closest("tr, article, li, .torrent, .result, .row, .gai") ?: anchor.parent() ?: anchor

    private fun titleFrom(container: Element, fallback: Element): String {
        val named = container.selectFirst(".detLink, .tLink, .torrent-name, .title, h2, h3")
            ?.text()?.trim().orEmpty()
        return named.ifBlank { fallback.text().trim() }
    }

    private fun firstDetailUrl(container: Element, baseUrl: String): String? =
        container.select("a[href]").firstNotNullOfOrNull { anchor ->
            val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@firstNotNullOfOrNull null
            href.takeIf { isDetailLink(it) }
        }

    private fun findTorrentLink(container: Element, baseUrl: String): String? =
        container.select("a[href]").firstNotNullOfOrNull { anchor ->
            val href = safeResolve(baseUrl, anchor.attr("href")) ?: return@firstNotNullOfOrNull null
            val path = runCatching { URI(href).rawPath.orEmpty().lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }.getOrDefault("")
            href.takeIf {
                path.endsWith(".torrent") || path.endsWith("/dl.php") || path.contains("download.php")
            }
        }

    private fun isDetailLink(value: String): Boolean {
        val uri = runCatching { URI(value) }.getOrNull() ?: return false
        val path = uri.rawPath.orEmpty().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        val query = uri.rawQuery.orEmpty().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        return path.contains("viewtopic.php") || path.contains("details.php") ||
            path.contains("/torrent/") || path.contains("/topic/") ||
            query.contains("id=") && (path.contains("browse") || path.contains("details"))
    }

    private fun safeResolve(baseUrl: String, value: String): String? {
        val clean = value.trim()
        if (clean.isBlank() || clean.startsWith("#") || clean.startsWith("javascript:", true)) return null
        if (clean.startsWith("magnet:", true)) return clean
        return runCatching {
            val resolved = URL(URL(baseUrl), clean).toString()
            resolved.takeIf { isSafeTrackerRequestUrl(it) }
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    private fun parseStats(row: Element): Triple<Long?, Int?, Int?> {
        val rowText = row.text().replace(',', '.')
        val sizeMatch = Regex(
            "(?i)([0-9]+(?:\\.[0-9]+)?)\\s*(байт|bytes|kb|kib|mb|mib|gb|gib|tb|tib|кб|мб|гб|тб)"
        ).find(rowText)
        val size = sizeMatch?.let { match ->
            val amount = match.groupValues[1].toDoubleOrNull() ?: return@let null
            val unit = match.groupValues[2].lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
            val multiplier = when (unit) {
                "байт", "bytes" -> 1.0
                "kb", "kib", "кб" -> 1024.0
                "mb", "mib", "мб" -> 1024.0 * 1024.0
                "gb", "gib", "гб" -> 1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0
                else -> 1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0
            }
            (amount * multiplier).takeIf { it <= Long.MAX_VALUE.toDouble() }?.toLong()
        }

        fun labelled(vararg patterns: String): Int? {
            for (pattern in patterns) {
                Regex(pattern, RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE).find(rowText)
                    ?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)?.toIntOrNull()?.let { if (it >= 0) return it }
            }
            return null
        }

        val seedsByLabel = labelled(
            "(?:seeders?|seeds?|сид(?:ы|ов)?|раздач(?:а|и))\\s*[:=]?\\s*([0-9]+)"
        )
        val peersByLabel = labelled(
            "(?:leechers?|peers?|пиров|лич(?:ей|и)?)\\s*[:=]?\\s*([0-9]+)"
        )
        val seedsByClass = row.selectFirst(
            ".seedmed, .seed, .seeders, .seed-count, .seeds, [class*=seed]"
        )?.text()?.filter(Char::isDigit)?.toIntOrNull()
        val peersByClass = row.selectFirst(
            ".leechmed, .leech, .leechers, .peer-count, .peers, [class*=leech]"
        )?.text()?.filter(Char::isDigit)?.toIntOrNull()
        val numericCells = row.select("td").mapNotNull { cell ->
            cell.text().replace(Regex("[\\s\\u00a0]"), "")
                .takeIf { it.matches(Regex("[0-9]{1,8}")) }?.toIntOrNull()
        }
        val seedsByPosition = numericCells.getOrNull(numericCells.size - 2)
        val peersByPosition = numericCells.lastOrNull()
        return Triple(
            size,
            seedsByLabel ?: seedsByClass ?: seedsByPosition,
            peersByLabel ?: peersByClass ?: peersByPosition
        )
    }

    private fun magnetInfoHash(magnet: String?): String? {
        if (magnet.isNullOrBlank()) return null
        val encoded = Regex("(?i)(?:[?&])xt=urn:btih:([^&]+)").find(magnet)
            ?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1) ?: return null
        val hash = runCatching {
            java.net.URLDecoder.decode(encoded, Charsets.UTF_8.name())
        }.getOrDefault(encoded).trim()
        return hash.takeIf { it.matches(Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-z2-7]{32})")) }
            ?.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
    }
}

package app.jacket.mobile.data

import java.net.URI
import java.net.URLEncoder

/**
 * Safe URL builder for user-configured website search links.
 * Results are opened in the external browser; arbitrary pages are not scraped.
 */
object WebSearchUrlBuilder {
    fun validateTemplate(template: String): String {
        val clean = template.trim()
        require(clean.isNotBlank()) { "Укажите URL поиска сайта" }
        require(clean.length <= MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH) { "URL шаблон слишком длинный" }
        require(clean.contains(QUERY_TOKEN)) { "В URL должен быть маркер {query}" }

        val probe = clean.replace(QUERY_TOKEN, "search-query")
        val uri = runCatching { URI(probe) }
            .getOrElse { throw IllegalArgumentException("Некорректный URL поиска", it) }
        require(uri.scheme.equals("https", ignoreCase = true)) {
            "Для сайта поиска требуется HTTPS"
        }
        require(!uri.host.isNullOrBlank() && uri.rawUserInfo == null) {
            "URL должен содержать домен и не должен содержать логин или пароль"
        }
        require(uri.fragment.isNullOrBlank()) { "Уберите фрагмент # из URL шаблона" }
        return clean
    }

    fun build(template: String, query: String): String {
        require(query.isNotBlank()) { "Введите название для поиска" }
        val clean = validateTemplate(template)
        val encoded = URLEncoder.encode(query.trim(), Charsets.UTF_8.name())
            .replace("+", "%20")
        val result = clean.replace(QUERY_TOKEN, encoded)
        require(result.length <= MAX_RESULT_LENGTH) { "Ссылка поиска слишком длинная" }
        val uri = URI(result)
        require(uri.scheme.equals("https", ignoreCase = true) &&
            !uri.host.isNullOrBlank() && uri.rawUserInfo == null
        ) { "Некорректная ссылка поиска" }
        return result
    }

    private const val QUERY_TOKEN = "{query}"
    private const val MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH = 2_048
    private const val MAX_RESULT_LENGTH = 4_096
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/data/WebSearchUrlBuilder.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

/** Strict Host header policy for the loopback-only media endpoint. */
object LocalStreamHostPolicy {
    fun accepts(hostHeader: String?, boundPort: Int): Boolean {
        if (hostHeader.isNullOrBlank() || boundPort !in 1..65535) return false
        val parts = hostHeader.trim().split(':', limit = 2)
        if (parts[0].trim().lowercase() != "127.0.0.1") return false
        if (parts.size == 1) return true
        val requestedPort = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: return false
        return requestedPort == boundPort
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/LocalStreamHostPolicy.kt

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/LocalStreamRoute.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

data class LocalStreamRoute(val token: String, val infoHash: String, val fileIndex: Int)

object LocalStreamRouteParser {
    private val tokenPattern = Regex("(?i)[a-f0-9]{32}")
    private val hashPattern = Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")

    fun parse(requestTarget: String): LocalStreamRoute? {
        if (requestTarget.isBlank() || requestTarget.contains('\\')) return null
        val path = requestTarget.substringBefore('?')
        if (path.contains("%2f", ignoreCase = true) || path.contains("%5c", ignoreCase = true)) return null
        val parts = path.split('/')
        if (parts.size != 5 || parts[0].isNotEmpty() || parts[1] != "stream") return null
        val token = parts[2].takeIf { tokenPattern.matches(it) } ?: return null
        val hash = parts[3].takeIf { hashPattern.matches(it) } ?: return null
        val indexText = parts[4]
        if (!indexText.matches(Regex("[0-9]{1,9}"))) return null
        val index = indexText.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
        return LocalStreamRoute(token.lowercase(), hash.lowercase(), index)
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/LocalStreamRoute.kt


========================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/LocalStreamServer.kt
========================================================================================

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.Closeable
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.security.SecureRandom
import java.util.concurrent.ArrayBlockingQueue
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadPoolExecutor
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * Loopback-only HTTP byte-range server for in-progress torrents. It never binds a LAN interface.
 * Every requested chunk is gated by libtorrent piece availability before reading the file.
 */
class LocalStreamServer(
    private val metadata: (String, Int) -> TorrentStreamTarget,
    private val awaitRange: (String, Int, Long, Long) -> File,
    cacheDirectory: File = File(System.getProperty("java.io.tmpdir") ?: ".", "jacket-stream-cache")
) : Closeable {
    private val streamCache = StreamDiskCache(cacheDirectory)
    @Volatile private var serverSocket: ServerSocket? = null
    @Volatile private var acceptThread: Thread? = null
    @Volatile private var workers: ExecutorService? = null
    @Volatile var port: Int = 0
        private set
    private val accessToken = newAccessToken()
    private val clients = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet<Socket>()

    val isRunning: Boolean get() = serverSocket?.isClosed == false

    @Synchronized
    fun start(): Int {
        if (isRunning) return port
        var bound: ServerSocket? = null
        var lastError: Exception? = null
        for (candidate in FIRST_PORT..LAST_PORT) {
            try {
                bound = ServerSocket(candidate, 32, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1")).apply {
                    reuseAddress = true
                    soTimeout = 1000
                }
                port = bound!!.localPort
                break
            } catch (error: Exception) {
                lastError = error
            }
        }
        serverSocket = bound ?: throw IllegalStateException(
            "Не удалось запустить локальный видеосервер на портах $FIRST_PORT–$LAST_PORT",
            lastError
        )
        workers = ThreadPoolExecutor(
            MAX_CLIENTS,
            MAX_CLIENTS,
            0L,
            TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS,
            ArrayBlockingQueue(MAX_QUEUED_CLIENTS)
        ) { runnable ->
            Thread(runnable, "JacketStreamClient").apply { isDaemon = true }
        }
        acceptThread = Thread({ acceptLoop() }, "JacketStreamAccept").apply {
            isDaemon = true
            start()
        }
        return port
    }

    fun url(infoHash: String, fileIndex: Int): String {
        require(infoHash.matches(Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")))
        require(fileIndex >= 0)
        check(isRunning) { "Локальный видеосервер не запущен" }
        return "http://127.0.0.1:$port/stream/$accessToken/${infoHash.lowercase()}/$fileIndex"
    }

    private fun acceptLoop() {
        while (isRunning) {
            try {
                val socket = serverSocket?.accept() ?: break
                socket.soTimeout = CLIENT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS
                clients.add(socket)
                try {
                    workers?.execute {
                        try {
                            handle(socket)
                        } finally {
                            clients.remove(socket)
                        }
                    } ?: run {
                        clients.remove(socket)
                        socket.close()
                    }
                } catch (_: java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException) {
                    clients.remove(socket)
                    socket.close()
                }
            } catch (_: java.net.SocketTimeoutException) {
                // Periodically re-check isRunning.
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                if (isRunning) continue else break
            }
        }
    }

    private fun handle(socket: Socket) {
        socket.use { client ->
            try {
                val input = BufferedInputStream(client.getInputStream())
                val requestLine = try {
                    readBoundedLine(input, MAX_REQUEST_LINE_BYTES)
                } catch (_: RequestTooLargeException) {
                    return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 431, "Request Header Fields Too Large")
                } ?: return
                val request = requestLine.split(' ')
                if (request.size != 3 || request[1].isBlank() ||
                    request[2] !in setOf("HTTP/1.0", "HTTP/1.1")
                ) {
                    return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 400, "Bad Request")
                }
                val method = request[0].uppercase()
                if (method != "GET" && method != "HEAD") {
                    return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 405, "Method Not Allowed", "Allow: GET, HEAD\r\n")
                }

                val headers = LinkedHashMap<String, String>()
                var totalHeaderBytes = requestLine.length + 2
                while (true) {
                    val line = try {
                        readBoundedLine(input, MAX_HEADER_LINE_BYTES)
                    } catch (_: RequestTooLargeException) {
                        return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 431, "Request Header Fields Too Large")
                    } ?: return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 400, "Bad Request")
                    totalHeaderBytes += line.length + 2
                    if (totalHeaderBytes > MAX_HEADER_BYTES) {
                        return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 431, "Request Header Fields Too Large")
                    }
                    if (line.isEmpty()) break
                    val separator = line.indexOf(':')
                    if (separator <= 0) return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 400, "Bad Request")
                    val name = line.substring(0, separator).trim()
                    val value = line.substring(separator + 1).trim()
                    if (name.isEmpty() || !name.all { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == '-' } ||
                        value.any { it.code < 0x20 && it != '\t' }
                    ) {
                        return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 400, "Bad Request")
                    }
                    headers[name.lowercase()] = value
                }

                if (!validHost(headers["host"])) return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 403, "Forbidden")
                val route = LocalStreamRouteParser.parse(request[1])
                    ?: return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 404, "Not Found")
                if (!validAccessToken(route.token)) return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 403, "Forbidden")
                val fileIndex = route.fileIndex
                val id = route.infoHash
                val target = try {
                    metadata(id, fileIndex)
                } catch (error: Exception) {
                    val notFound = error.message?.contains("не найден", true) == true
                    return sendError(
                        client.getOutputStream(),
                        if (notFound) 404 else 503,
                        if (notFound) "Not Found" else "Stream unavailable"
                    )
                }
                if (target.sizeBytes <= 0L) return sendError(client.getOutputStream(), 416, "Range Not Satisfiable")

                val range = try {
                    RangeParser.parse(headers["range"], target.sizeBytes)
                } catch (_: IllegalArgumentException) {
                    val out = client.getOutputStream()
                    writeHeaders(out, 416, "Range Not Satisfiable", listOf(
                        "Content-Range: bytes */${target.sizeBytes}",
                        "Accept-Ranges: bytes",
                        "Content-Length: 0"
                    ))
                    return
                }
                val response = RangeResponseBuilder.build(range, target.sizeBytes, contentType(target.name))
                val start = response.start
                val end = response.endInclusive
                val out = client.getOutputStream()
                if (method == "HEAD") {
                    writeHeaders(out, response.statusCode, response.reason, response.headers)
                    return
                }

                // Prime the first bytes before committing a 200/206 response.
                // If the swarm cannot provide the requested piece, the player
                // receives a retryable HTTP error instead of a truncated success.
                val firstCount = minOf(STREAM_CHUNK_BYTES, end - start + 1L).toInt()
                val firstBytes = try {
                    readChunk(id, fileIndex, start, firstCount)
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    return sendError(
                        out,
                        503,
                        "Service Unavailable",
                        "Retry-After: 2\r\n"
                    )
                }

                writeHeaders(out, response.statusCode, response.reason, response.headers)
                out.write(firstBytes)
                out.flush()

                var offset = start + firstBytes.size
                while (offset <= end) {
                    val count = minOf(STREAM_CHUNK_BYTES, end - offset + 1L).toInt()
                    if (count <= 0) throw java.io.IOException("Пустой диапазон видеопотока")
                    val bytes = readChunk(id, fileIndex, offset, count)
                    out.write(bytes)
                    out.flush()
                    offset += bytes.size
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // The player will reconnect on a failed range; do not expose internal file paths.
            }
        }
    }

    /** Loads a bounded byte segment, treating cache failures as cache misses. */
    private fun readChunk(id: String, fileIndex: Int, offset: Long, count: Int): ByteArray {
        require(count > 0) { "Пустой диапазон видеопотока" }
        val key = StreamCacheKey(id, fileIndex, offset)
        val cached = runCatching { streamCache.get(key, count) }.getOrNull()
        return cached?.bytes ?: run {
            val file = awaitRange(id, fileIndex, offset, count.toLong())
            ByteArray(count).also { buffer ->
                java.io.RandomAccessFile(file, "r").use { input ->
                    input.seek(offset)
                    input.readFully(buffer)
                }
                // The verified torrent file remains authoritative. A full or
                // failed cache must never prevent playback.
                runCatching { streamCache.put(key, buffer) }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Reads one HTTP line without allowing BufferedReader.readLine() to allocate an
     * attacker-controlled amount of memory before the header-size check runs.
     */
    private fun readBoundedLine(input: InputStream, maxBytes: Int): String? {
        val bytes = ByteArrayOutputStream(minOf(maxBytes, 256))
        var received = false
        while (true) {
            val next = input.read()
            if (next < 0) {
                if (!received) return null
                break
            }
            received = true
            if (next == '\n'.code) break
            if (bytes.size() >= maxBytes) throw RequestTooLargeException()
            bytes.write(next)
        }
        val raw = bytes.toByteArray()
        val length = if (raw.lastOrNull() == '\r'.code.toByte()) raw.size - 1 else raw.size
        return String(raw, 0, length, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
    }

    private class RequestTooLargeException : IOException()

    private fun validHost(value: String?): Boolean = LocalStreamHostPolicy.accepts(value, port)

    private fun validAccessToken(candidate: String): Boolean =
        MessageDigest.isEqual(
            accessToken.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII),
            candidate.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
        )

    private fun contentType(filename: String): String = when (filename.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()) {
        "mp4", "m4v" -> "video/mp4"
        "mkv" -> "video/x-matroska"
        "webm" -> "video/webm"
        "mov" -> "video/quicktime"
        "avi" -> "video/x-msvideo"
        "mpeg", "mpg" -> "video/mpeg"
        "ts" -> "video/mp2t"
        "m3u8" -> "application/vnd.apple.mpegurl"
        "mp3" -> "audio/mpeg"
        "aac" -> "audio/aac"
        "flac" -> "audio/flac"
        "srt" -> "application/x-subrip"
        "vtt" -> "text/vtt"
        else -> "application/octet-stream"
    }

    private fun writeHeaders(out: OutputStream, code: Int, reason: String, headers: List<String>) {
        val text = buildString {
            append("HTTP/1.1 $code $reason\r\n")
            headers.forEach { append(it).append("\r\n") }
            append("\r\n")
        }
        out.write(text.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII))
        out.flush()
    }

    private fun sendError(socket: OutputStream, code: Int, reason: String, extra: String = "") {
        val body = reason.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
        val headers = buildString {
            append("HTTP/1.1 $code $reason\r\n")
            append("Connection: close\r\n")
            append("Content-Type: text/plain; charset=utf-8\r\n")
            append("Content-Length: ${body.size}\r\n")
            append(extra)
            append("\r\n")
        }
        socket.write(headers.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII))
        socket.write(body)
        socket.flush()
    }

    @Synchronized
    override fun close() {
        try { serverSocket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) { }
        serverSocket = null
        clients.forEach { client -> runCatching { client.close() } }
        clients.clear()
        try { workers?.shutdownNow() } catch (_: Exception) { }
        workers = null
        runCatching { streamCache.close() }
        port = 0
        acceptThread = null
    }

    companion object {
        private fun newAccessToken(): String {
            val bytes = ByteArray(16)
            SecureRandom().nextBytes(bytes)
            return bytes.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
        }

        private const val FIRST_PORT = 8090
        private const val LAST_PORT = 8099
        private const val MAX_CLIENTS = 4
        private const val MAX_QUEUED_CLIENTS = 8
        private const val MAX_REQUEST_LINE_BYTES = 4 * 1024
        private const val MAX_HEADER_LINE_BYTES = 8 * 1024
        private const val MAX_HEADER_BYTES = 16 * 1024
        private const val CLIENT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 35_000
        private const val STREAM_CHUNK_BYTES = StreamDiskCache.DEFAULT_SEGMENT_BYTES.toLong()
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/LocalStreamServer.kt

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/PlaybackProgressStore.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import android.content.Context
import androidx.core.content.edit
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.security.MessageDigest

/** Local playback positions; keys are opaque and do not persist source URLs or authentication data. */
class PlaybackProgressStore(context: Context) {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val preferences = appContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFERENCES, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    fun position(key: String): Long = preferences.getLong(key, 0L).coerceAtLeast(0L)

    fun save(key: String, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        if (key.isBlank()) return
        val position = positionMs.coerceAtLeast(0L)
        if (durationMs > 0L && position >= durationMs * WATCHED_PERCENT / 100L) {
            preferences.edit { remove(key) }
        } else if (position > 0L) {
            preferences.edit { putLong(key, position) }
        }
        updateTorrentSession(key, position, durationMs)
    }

    fun clear(key: String) {
        preferences.edit { remove(key) }
        torrentIdentity(key)?.let { (hash, index) -> PlaybackSessionStore(appContext).remove(hash, index) }
    }

    private fun updateTorrentSession(key: String, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        torrentIdentity(key)?.let { (hash, index) ->
            PlaybackSessionStore(appContext).saveProgress(hash, index, positionMs, durationMs)
        }
    }

    private fun torrentIdentity(key: String): Pair<String, Int>? {
        if (!key.startsWith("torrent:")) return null
        val parts = key.removePrefix("torrent:").split(':')
        if (parts.size != 2 || !parts[0].matches(INFO_HASH)) return null
        val index = parts[1].toIntOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= 0 } ?: return null
        return parts[0].lowercase() to index
    }

    companion object {
        private const val PREFERENCES = "jacket_playback_positions"
        private const val WATCHED_PERCENT = 90L
        private val INFO_HASH = Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")

        fun torrentKey(infoHash: String, fileIndex: Int): String = "torrent:${infoHash.lowercase()}:$fileIndex"

        fun urlKey(url: String): String {
            val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(url.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            val hex = digest.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
            return "url:$hex"
        }
    }
}


/** Resumable local-torrent viewing history. Kept separate from the card-opening history. */
data class PlaybackSession(
    val infoHash: String,
    val fileIndex: Int,
    val title: String,
    val positionMs: Long,
    val durationMs: Long,
    val updatedAt: Long
) {
    val key: String get() = "$infoHash:$fileIndex"
    val progress: Float
        get() = if (durationMs <= 0L) 0f else
            (positionMs.toFloat() / durationMs.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
}

class PlaybackSessionStore(context: Context) {
    private val preferences = context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(SESSION_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    val sessions: StateFlow<List<PlaybackSession>> get() = state.asStateFlow()

    init { synchronized(lock) { publish(readLocked()) } }

    fun begin(infoHash: String, fileIndex: Int, title: String, initialPositionMs: Long) {
        require(infoHash.matches(INFO_HASH) && fileIndex >= 0)
        synchronized(lock) {
            val current = readLocked()
            val previous = current.firstOrNull { it.infoHash == infoHash.lowercase() && it.fileIndex == fileIndex }
            val session = PlaybackSession(
                infoHash.lowercase(), fileIndex, title.substringAfterLast('/').take(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH).ifBlank { "Видео" },
                previous?.positionMs ?: initialPositionMs.coerceAtLeast(0L),
                previous?.durationMs ?: 0L,
                System.currentTimeMillis()
            )
            writeLocked((current.filterNot { it.key == session.key } + session)
                .sortedByDescending { it.updatedAt }.take(MAX_SESSIONS))
        }
    }

    fun saveProgress(infoHash: String, fileIndex: Int, positionMs: Long, durationMs: Long) {
        if (!infoHash.matches(INFO_HASH) || fileIndex < 0) return
        synchronized(lock) {
            val current = readLocked()
            val old = current.firstOrNull { it.infoHash == infoHash.lowercase() && it.fileIndex == fileIndex } ?: return
            val position = positionMs.coerceAtLeast(0L)
            val duration = durationMs.takeIf { it > 0L } ?: old.durationMs
            val next = if (duration > 0L && position >= duration * WATCHED_PERCENT / 100L) {
                current.filterNot { it.key == old.key }
            } else {
                current.filterNot { it.key == old.key } + old.copy(
                    positionMs = position, durationMs = duration, updatedAt = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
            }
            writeLocked(next.sortedByDescending { it.updatedAt }.take(MAX_SESSIONS))
        }
    }

    fun remove(infoHash: String, fileIndex: Int) = synchronized(lock) {
        writeLocked(readLocked().filterNot { it.infoHash == infoHash.lowercase() && it.fileIndex == fileIndex })
    }

    private fun readLocked(): List<PlaybackSession> = runCatching {
        val array = JSONArray(preferences.getString(SESSION_KEY, "[]").orEmpty())
        buildList {
            for (index in 0 until array.length()) {
                val item = array.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
                val hash = item.optString("infoHash").lowercase()
                val fileIndex = item.optInt("fileIndex", -1)
                if (!hash.matches(INFO_HASH) || fileIndex < 0) continue
                add(
                    PlaybackSession(
                        hash, fileIndex, item.optString("title").take(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH).ifBlank { "Видео" },
                        item.optLong("positionMs", 0L).coerceAtLeast(0L),
                        item.optLong("durationMs", 0L).coerceAtLeast(0L),
                        item.optLong("updatedAt", 0L).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                    )
                )
            }
        }.distinctBy { it.key }.sortedByDescending { it.updatedAt }.take(MAX_SESSIONS)
    }.getOrDefault(emptyList())

    private fun writeLocked(items: List<PlaybackSession>) {
        val array = JSONArray()
        items.forEach { item ->
            array.put(
                JSONObject().put("infoHash", item.infoHash).put("fileIndex", item.fileIndex)
                    .put("title", item.title).put("positionMs", item.positionMs)
                    .put("durationMs", item.durationMs).put("updatedAt", item.updatedAt)
            )
        }
        preferences.edit { putString(SESSION_KEY, array.toString()) }
        publish(items)
    }

    private fun publish(items: List<PlaybackSession>) { state.value = items }

    companion object {
        private const val SESSION_PREFS = "jacket_playback_sessions"
        private const val SESSION_KEY = "sessions_json"
        private const val MAX_SESSIONS = 30
        private const val MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 240
        private const val WATCHED_PERCENT = 90L
        private val INFO_HASH = Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")
        private val lock = Any()
        private val state = MutableStateFlow<List<PlaybackSession>>(emptyList())
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/PlaybackProgressStore.kt

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/RangeParser.kt
========================================================================================

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

/** Inclusive byte range used by the local HTTP stream endpoint. */
data class ByteRange(val start: Long, val endInclusive: Long) {
    val length: Long get() = endInclusive - start + 1L
}

/** Strict single-range parser. Multipart ranges are intentionally not supported. */
object RangeParser {
    fun parse(header: String?, size: Long): ByteRange? {
        require(size >= 0L)
        if (header.isNullOrBlank()) return null
        if (!header.startsWith("bytes=", ignoreCase = true)) throw IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported Range unit")
        val value = header.substringAfter('=').trim()
        if (value.isBlank() || value.contains(',')) throw IllegalArgumentException("Only one byte range is supported")
        val pieces = value.split('-', limit = 2)
        if (pieces.size != 2) throw IllegalArgumentException("Malformed Range")
        val startPart = pieces[0].trim()
        val endPart = pieces[1].trim()
        if (size == 0L) throw IllegalArgumentException("Empty file")

        val range = if (startPart.isBlank()) {
            val suffix = endPart.toLongOrNull()?.takeIf { it > 0L }
                ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Invalid suffix range")
            val actual = minOf(suffix, size)
            ByteRange(size - actual, size - 1L)
        } else {
            val start = startPart.toLongOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= 0L }
                ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Invalid range start")
            if (start >= size) throw IllegalArgumentException("Range starts beyond end of file")
            val end = if (endPart.isBlank()) size - 1L else
                endPart.toLongOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= start }
                    ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Invalid range end")
            ByteRange(start, minOf(end, size - 1L))
        }
        return range
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/RangeParser.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

data class RangeHttpResponse(
    val statusCode: Int,
    val reason: String,
    val start: Long,
    val endInclusive: Long,
    val contentLength: Long,
    val headers: List<String>
)

/** Pure HTTP range response builder shared by the loopback server and unit tests. */
object RangeResponseBuilder {
    fun build(range: ByteRange?, size: Long, contentType: String): RangeHttpResponse {
        require(size > 0L) { "Range response requires a non-empty file" }
        val start = range?.start ?: 0L
        val end = range?.endInclusive ?: (size - 1L)
        require(start >= 0L && end >= start && end < size)
        val partial = range != null
        val headers = buildList {
            add("Content-Type: $contentType")
            add("Accept-Ranges: bytes")
            add("Content-Length: ${end - start + 1L}")
            add("Connection: close")
            add("Cache-Control: no-store")
            if (partial) add("Content-Range: bytes $start-$end/$size")
        }
        return RangeHttpResponse(
            statusCode = if (partial) 206 else 200,
            reason = if (partial) "Partial Content" else "OK",
            start = start,
            endInclusive = end,
            contentLength = end - start + 1L,
            headers = headers
        )
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/StreamDiskCache.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import java.io.Closeable
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.RandomAccessFile
import java.util.LinkedHashMap

/** Stable identity for one cached byte interval of one torrent file. */
internal data class StreamCacheKey(
    val infoHash: String,
    val fileIndex: Int,
    val startOffset: Long
)

internal data class CachedStreamRange(
    val startOffset: Long,
    val bytes: ByteArray
)

/**
 * A bounded LRU disk ring for recently served video segments.
 *
 * The cache stores only playback copies. Libtorrent's verified torrent files
 * remain untouched, so evicting a cache slot never invalidates resume data or
 * causes a previously downloaded torrent piece to be downloaded again.
 */
internal class StreamDiskCache(
    directory: File,
    private val maxBytes: Long = DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES,
    val segmentBytes: Int = DEFAULT_SEGMENT_BYTES
) : Closeable {
    private data class Slot(val index: Int, val length: Int)

    private val slotCount: Int
    private val entries = LinkedHashMap<StreamCacheKey, Slot>(16, 0.75f, true)
    private val slotOwners: Array<StreamCacheKey?>
    private val cacheFile: File
    private val storage: RandomAccessFile
    private var closed = false

    var evictions: Long = 0L
        private set

    init {
        require(maxBytes >= segmentBytes.toLong()) { "Кэш должен содержать хотя бы один сегмент" }
        require(segmentBytes > 0) { "Размер сегмента должен быть положительным" }
        slotCount = (maxBytes / segmentBytes).coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt()
        require(slotCount > 0) { "Недостаточный размер дискового кэша" }
        slotOwners = arrayOfNulls(slotCount)
        require(directory.mkdirs() || directory.isDirectory) { "Не удалось создать каталог дискового кэша" }
        cacheFile = File.createTempFile("jacket-stream-", ".ring", directory)
        storage = RandomAccessFile(cacheFile, "rw")
    }

    /**
     * Returns the cached bytes beginning at the requested offset. The returned
     * interval may be shorter than maxLength when it reaches the end of a slot.
     */
    @Synchronized
    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun get(key: StreamCacheKey, maxLength: Int): CachedStreamRange? {
        checkOpen()
        if (maxLength !in 1..segmentBytes || key.startOffset < 0L) return null

        // Most-recently-used entries are examined first. A seek into an already
        // cached interval can reuse its suffix, even if the new Range starts at
        // a different byte offset.
        val candidates = entries.entries.toList().asReversed()
        for (candidate in candidates) {
            val storedKey = candidate.key
            if (storedKey.infoHash != key.infoHash || storedKey.fileIndex != key.fileIndex) continue
            if (key.startOffset < storedKey.startOffset) continue
            val delta = key.startOffset - storedKey.startOffset
            if (delta >= candidate.value.length.toLong()) continue

            val length = minOf(maxLength, candidate.value.length - delta.toInt())
            if (length <= 0) continue
            val result = ByteArray(length)
            storage.seek(candidate.value.index.toLong() * segmentBytes + delta)
            storage.readFully(result)
            entries[storedKey] // Refresh LRU position.
            return CachedStreamRange(key.startOffset, result)
        }
        return null
    }

    /** Stores one segment and evicts the least-recently-used slot when the ring is full. */
    @Synchronized
    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun put(key: StreamCacheKey, data: ByteArray) {
        checkOpen()
        require(data.isNotEmpty() && data.size <= segmentBytes) { "Недопустимый размер сегмента" }

        val existing = entries[key]
        val slotIndex = existing?.index ?: allocateSlot()
        storage.seek(slotIndex.toLong() * segmentBytes)
        storage.write(data)

        val previousOwner = slotOwners[slotIndex]
        if (previousOwner != null && previousOwner != key) entries.remove(previousOwner)
        entries[key] = Slot(slotIndex, data.size)
        slotOwners[slotIndex] = key
    }

    @Synchronized
    override fun close() {
        if (closed) return
        closed = true
        entries.clear()
        slotOwners.fill(null)
        runCatching { storage.close() }
        runCatching { cacheFile.delete() }
    }

    private fun allocateSlot(): Int {
        if (entries.size < slotCount) {
            val free = slotOwners.indexOfFirst { it == null }
            if (free >= 0) return free
        }
        val leastRecent = entries.entries.iterator()
        if (!leastRecent.hasNext()) return 0
        val evicted = leastRecent.next()
        val slot = evicted.value.index
        entries.remove(evicted.key)
        slotOwners[slot] = null
        evictions++
        return slot
    }

    private fun checkOpen() {
        check(!closed) { "Дисковый кэш потока уже закрыт" }
    }

    companion object {
        const val DEFAULT_SEGMENT_BYTES = 256 * 1024
        const val DEFAULT_MAX_BYTES = 32L * 1024L * 1024L
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/StreamDiskCache.kt

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/StreamPiecePlanner.kt
========================================================================================

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

/** One piece deadline relative to the first requested piece. */
internal data class StreamPieceDeadline(
    val pieceIndex: Int,
    val deadlineMs: Int
)

/** Bounded, ordered piece window for a video byte-range request. */
internal data class StreamPiecePlan(
    val firstPiece: Int,
    val lastPiece: Int,
    val deadlines: List<StreamPieceDeadline>
)

/**
 * Maps a file-relative HTTP byte range to torrent piece indices.
 *
 * The requested piece gets the nearest deadline. Subsequent pieces receive
 * increasing deadlines, so libtorrent favours the playback position before
 * the forward buffer without enabling global sequential-download mode.
 */
internal object StreamPiecePlanner {
    fun plan(
        fileOffset: Long,
        fileSize: Long,
        pieceLength: Long,
        requestStart: Long,
        requestLength: Long,
        firstFilePiece: Int,
        lastFilePiece: Int,
        prefetchBytes: Long = DEFAULT_PREFETCH_BYTES,
        maxPieces: Int = DEFAULT_MAX_PIECES,
        deadlineStepMs: Int = DEFAULT_DEADLINE_STEP_MS
    ): StreamPiecePlan {
        require(fileOffset >= 0L) { "Отрицательное смещение файла" }
        require(fileSize > 0L) { "Пустой файл" }
        require(pieceLength > 0L) { "Некорректный размер части торрента" }
        require(requestStart >= 0L && requestLength > 0L) { "Некорректный HTTP-диапазон" }
        require(requestStart < fileSize && requestLength <= fileSize - requestStart) {
            "HTTP-диапазон выходит за пределы файла"
        }
        require(firstFilePiece >= 0 && lastFilePiece >= firstFilePiece) {
            "Некорректные границы файла в торренте"
        }
        require(prefetchBytes >= 0L && maxPieces > 0 && deadlineStepMs >= 0)

        val absoluteStart = Math.addExact(fileOffset, requestStart)
        val requestedEndExclusive = Math.addExact(requestStart, requestLength)
        val prefetchEndExclusive = if (prefetchBytes > fileSize - requestedEndExclusive) {
            fileSize
        } else {
            requestedEndExclusive + prefetchBytes
        }
        val absoluteEndInclusive = Math.addExact(
            fileOffset,
            prefetchEndExclusive - 1L
        )

        val first = (absoluteStart / pieceLength).toInt().coerceAtLeast(firstFilePiece)
        val last = minOf(
            (absoluteEndInclusive / pieceLength).toInt(),
            lastFilePiece,
            first + maxPieces - 1
        )
        require(first in firstFilePiece..lastFilePiece && last >= first) {
            "Не удалось сопоставить диапазон с частями торрента"
        }

        val deadlines = (first..last).mapIndexed { position, piece ->
            StreamPieceDeadline(
                pieceIndex = piece,
                deadlineMs = (position.toLong() * deadlineStepMs)
                    .coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong())
                    .toInt()
            )
        }
        return StreamPiecePlan(first, last, deadlines)
    }

    const val DEFAULT_PREFETCH_BYTES = 8L * 1024L * 1024L
    const val DEFAULT_MAX_PIECES = 32
    const val DEFAULT_DEADLINE_STEP_MS = 350
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/StreamPiecePlanner.kt
