package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.ServiceCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import app.jacket.mobile.R
import app.jacket.mobile.MainActivity
import app.jacket.mobile.data.MediaSettingsStore
import app.jacket.mobile.data.IndexerProtocol
import app.jacket.mobile.data.IndexerErrorSanitizer
import app.jacket.mobile.data.LocalNetwork
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.isActive
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL
import java.net.URI
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

/** Single-process foreground owner of the BitTorrent session and loopback media server. */
class TorrentDownloadService : Service() {
    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)
    private var pollingStarted = false
    private val commandMutex = Mutex()
    private val playbackActive = AtomicBoolean(false)
    private var streamServer: LocalStreamServer? = null
    private var streamingTorrentId: String? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        createChannel()
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        val action = intent?.action ?: ACTION_RESTORE
        startForegroundFor(playbackActive.get() || action == ACTION_PLAY)
        serviceScope.launch {
            commandMutex.withLock {
            try {
                withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    TorrentEngine.start(applicationContext)
                    when (action) {
                        ACTION_ADD -> {
                            val magnet = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_MAGNET).orEmpty()
                            val title = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TITLE).orEmpty()
                            require(magnet.startsWith("magnet:?")) { "Для загрузки нужна magnet-ссылка" }
                            TorrentEngine.add(applicationContext, magnet, title)
                        }
                        ACTION_ADD_TORRENT -> {
                            val path = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TORRENT_PATH).orEmpty()
                            require(path.isNotBlank()) { "Путь к .torrent не указан" }
                            TorrentEngine.addTorrent(applicationContext, File(path), intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TITLE))
                        }
                        ACTION_ADD_TORRENT_URL -> {
                            val url = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TORRENT_URL).orEmpty()
                            val title = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TITLE).orEmpty()
                            val file = downloadTorrentFile(
                                url,
                                title,
                                intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_INDEXER_ID)
                            )
                            TorrentEngine.addTorrent(applicationContext, file, title)
                        }
                        ACTION_PAUSE -> TorrentEngine.pause(applicationContext, intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty())
                        ACTION_RESUME -> TorrentEngine.resume(applicationContext, intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty())
                        ACTION_SELECT_FILE -> TorrentEngine.selectFile(
                            applicationContext,
                            intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty(),
                            intent?.getIntExtra(EXTRA_FILE_INDEX, -1) ?: -1
                        )
                        ACTION_REMOVE -> TorrentEngine.remove(
                            applicationContext,
                            intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty(),
                            intent?.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_DELETE_FILES, false) ?: false
                        )
                        ACTION_PLAY -> startTorrentPlayback(
                            intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty(),
                            intent?.getIntExtra(EXTRA_FILE_INDEX, -1) ?: -1
                        )
                        ACTION_STOP_PLAYBACK -> stopTorrentPlayback()
                        ACTION_PAUSE_SELECTED -> {
                            val id = intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_ID).orEmpty()
                            if (TorrentEngine.snapshots.value.firstOrNull { it.id == id }?.paused == true) {
                                TorrentEngine.resume(applicationContext, id)
                            } else {
                                TorrentEngine.pause(applicationContext, id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    TorrentEngine.refresh()
                }
                startPolling()
            } catch (cancelled: CancellationException) {
                throw cancelled
            } catch (error: Exception) {
                getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
                    .notify(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification("Ошибка: ${error.message ?: "не удалось выполнить операцию"}", null))
            }
            }
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private suspend fun startTorrentPlayback(id: String, fileIndex: Int) {
        require(id.matches(Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})"))) { "Некорректный infohash" }
        require(fileIndex >= 0) { "Не выбран файл раздачи" }
        val target = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            if (TorrentQueueStore(applicationContext).isPaused(id)) {
                TorrentEngine.resume(applicationContext, id)
            }
            if (streamingTorrentId != null && streamingTorrentId != id) {
                TorrentEngine.clearStreamPriorities(streamingTorrentId!!)
            }
            selectFileWhenReady(id, fileIndex)
            val server = streamServer ?: LocalStreamServer(
                metadata = TorrentEngine::target,
                awaitRange = { hash, index, start, length ->
                    try {
                        TorrentEngine.awaitRange(hash, index, start, length)
                    } catch (error: Exception) {
                        TorrentEngine.reportError(hash, error.message ?: "Ошибка локального видеопотока")
                        throw error
                    }
                },
                cacheDirectory = File(applicationContext.cacheDir, "torrent-stream")
            ).also {
                it.start()
                streamServer = it
                TorrentEngine.setLocalStreamPort(it.port)
            }
            streamingTorrentId = id
            val item = TorrentEngine.target(id, fileIndex)
            val subtitles = TorrentEngine.subtitleTargets(id, fileIndex).map { subtitle ->
                val language = subtitle.name.substringAfterLast('/').substringBeforeLast('.')
                    .substringAfterLast('.')
                    .takeIf { it.matches(Regex("(?i)[a-z]{2,3}")) }
                PlaybackSubtitle(
                    url = server.url(id, subtitle.fileIndex),
                    mimeType = if (subtitle.name.endsWith(".vtt", true)) "text/vtt" else "application/x-subrip",
                    language = language,
                    label = subtitle.name.substringAfterLast('/')
                )
            }
            Triple(server.url(id, fileIndex), item, subtitles)
        }
        val key = PlaybackProgressStore.torrentKey(id, fileIndex)
        val savedPosition = PlaybackProgressStore(applicationContext).position(key)
        PlaybackSessionStore(applicationContext).begin(id, fileIndex, target.second.name, savedPosition)
        playbackActive.set(true)
        startForegroundFor(mediaPlayback = true, status = "Смотрим: ${target.second.name}")
        TorrentPlaybackBus.publish(
            StreamPlaybackRequest(
                title = target.second.name,
                url = target.first,
                progressKey = key,
                initialPositionMs = savedPosition,
                subtitles = target.third
            )
        )
    }

    private suspend fun selectFileWhenReady(id: String, fileIndex: Int) {
        var lastError: Exception? = null
        repeat(60) {
            try {
                TorrentEngine.selectFile(applicationContext, id, fileIndex)
                return
            } catch (error: IllegalStateException) {
                val message = error.message.orEmpty().lowercase()
                val retryable = "подключается" in message || "метаданные" in message ||
                    ("не найден" in message && TorrentQueueStore(applicationContext).get(id) != null)
                if (!retryable) throw error
                lastError = error
                delay(500)
            }
        }
        throw java.net.SocketTimeoutException(
            "Не удалось получить метаданные раздачи за 30 секунд: ${lastError?.message.orEmpty()}"
        )
    }

    private suspend fun stopTorrentPlayback() {
        playbackActive.set(false)
        TorrentPlaybackBus.publish(null)
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            streamingTorrentId?.let(TorrentEngine::clearStreamPriorities)
            streamingTorrentId = null
            streamServer?.close()
            streamServer = null
            TorrentEngine.setLocalStreamPort(null)
        }
        startForegroundFor(mediaPlayback = false, status = "Загрузка продолжается в фоне")
    }

    private fun startPolling() {
        if (pollingStarted) return
        pollingStarted = true
        serviceScope.launch {
            var lastResumeSave = 0L
            while (isActive) {
                commandMutex.withLock {
                    withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                        TorrentEngine.restorePending(applicationContext)
                        TorrentEngine.refresh()
                        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                        if (now - lastResumeSave >= RESUME_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS) {
                            TorrentEngine.saveAllResumeData()
                            lastResumeSave = now
                        }
                    }
                }
                updateNotification()
                delay(POLL_INTERVAL_MS)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun updateNotification() {
        val rows = TorrentEngine.snapshots.value
        val activeStream = TorrentPlaybackBus.active.value
        val selected = rows.firstOrNull { it.files.any { f -> f.isSelected } }
        val down = rows.sumOf { it.downloadBytesPerSecond.toLong() }
        val up = rows.sumOf { it.uploadBytesPerSecond.toLong() }
        val text = when {
            activeStream != null && selected != null -> {
                val file = selected.files.firstOrNull { it.isSelected }
                "${activeStream.title} • ${(file?.progress ?: 0f).times(100).toInt()}% • ↓ ${formatRate(down)}"
            }
            rows.isEmpty() -> "Нет активных загрузок"
            else -> "${rows.size} задач · ↓ ${formatRate(down)} · ↑ ${formatRate(up)}"
        }
        val pauseId = selected?.id ?: rows.firstOrNull { !it.paused }?.id
        getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            .notify(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification(text, pauseId))
    }

    private fun startForegroundFor(mediaPlayback: Boolean, status: String = "Запуск торрент-движка…") {
        val notification = notification(status, TorrentEngine.snapshots.value.firstOrNull { !it.paused }?.id)
        val type = if (mediaPlayback) {
            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK
        } else {
            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC
        }
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            ServiceCompat.startForeground(this, NOTIFICATION_ID, notification, type)
        } else {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
        }
    }

    private fun downloadTorrentFile(urlValue: String, title: String, indexerId: String?): File {
        val settingsStore = MediaSettingsStore(applicationContext)
        val sourceConfig = indexerId?.let { wanted ->
            settingsStore.indexers().firstOrNull { it.id == wanted }
        }
        val trackerCookie = indexerId?.let { wanted ->
            settingsStore.webSearchProviders().firstOrNull { it.id == wanted }?.cookie
        }.orEmpty()
        val sourceCookie = sourceConfig?.cookie?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: trackerCookie
        var currentUrl = urlValue
        val originalUri = Uri.parse(urlValue)
        require(LocalNetwork.isAllowedUrl(urlValue)) {
            "Для torrent-файла нужен HTTPS или HTTP-адрес локального сервера"
        }
        val keyParameter = if (sourceConfig?.protocol == IndexerProtocol.JACKETT) {
            "jackett_apikey"
        } else "apikey"
        if (sourceConfig != null && sourceConfig.apiKey.isNotBlank()) {
            val uri = Uri.parse(currentUrl)
            currentUrl = if (uri.getQueryParameter(keyParameter) == null) {
                uri.buildUpon().appendQueryParameter(keyParameter, sourceConfig.apiKey).build().toString()
            } else currentUrl
        }

        val dir = File(filesDir, "torrent-imports").apply { mkdirs() }
        val safeName = title.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]"), "_").take(64).ifBlank { "release" }
        val target = File.createTempFile("${safeName}_", ".torrent", dir)
        var activeUrl = currentUrl
        var redirectCount = 0
        try {
            while (true) {
                val uri = Uri.parse(activeUrl)
                val allowedRedirect = when (uri.scheme?.lowercase()) {
                    "https" -> LocalNetwork.isAllowedUrl(activeUrl)
                    "http" -> originalUri.scheme.equals("http", true) &&
                        uri.host.equals(originalUri.host, ignoreCase = true) &&
                        effectivePort(uri) == effectivePort(originalUri) &&
                        LocalNetwork.isAllowedUrl(activeUrl)
                    else -> false
                }
                require(allowedRedirect && uri.userInfo == null && !uri.host.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    "Перенаправление torrent-файла ведёт на небезопасный адрес"
                }
                val sameOrigin = uri.scheme.equals(originalUri.scheme, true) &&
                    uri.host.equals(originalUri.host, ignoreCase = true) &&
                    effectivePort(uri) == effectivePort(originalUri)
                val requestUrl = if (
                    sameOrigin && sourceConfig?.apiKey?.isNotBlank() == true &&
                    uri.getQueryParameter(keyParameter) == null
                ) {
                    uri.buildUpon().appendQueryParameter(keyParameter, sourceConfig.apiKey).build().toString()
                } else activeUrl
                val connection = (URL(requestUrl).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
                    connectTimeout = 10_000
                    readTimeout = 25_000
                    instanceFollowRedirects = false
                    setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "JacketAndroid/2.1")
                    if (sameOrigin) {
                        if (sourceConfig != null) {
                            if (sourceConfig.username.isNotBlank() || sourceConfig.password.isNotBlank()) {
                                val credentials = "${sourceConfig.username}:${sourceConfig.password}"
                                val encoded = android.util.Base64.encodeToString(
                                    credentials.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8),
                                    android.util.Base64.NO_WRAP
                                )
                                setRequestProperty("Authorization", "Basic $encoded")
                            }
                            if (sourceConfig.apiKey.isNotBlank() && sourceConfig.protocol != IndexerProtocol.JACKETT) {
                                setRequestProperty("X-Api-Key", sourceConfig.apiKey.take(512))
                            }
                        }
                        if (sourceCookie.isNotBlank()) setRequestProperty("Cookie", sourceCookie.take(4096))
                    }
                }
                try {
                    val code = connection.responseCode
                    if (code in REDIRECT_CODES) {
                        if (redirectCount++ >= MAX_TORRENT_REDIRECTS) throw IOException("Слишком много перенаправлений .torrent")
                        val location = connection.getHeaderField("Location")
                            ?: throw IOException("Сервер вернул перенаправление без Location")
                        activeUrl = URL(activeUrl).toURI().resolve(URI(location)).toString()
                        continue
                    }
                    if (code !in 200..299) throw IOException("HTTP $code при загрузке .torrent")
                    val declaredLength = connection.getHeaderFieldLong("Content-Length", -1L)
                    if (declaredLength > TorrentQueueStore.MAX_TORRENT_BYTES) {
                        throw IOException("Файл .torrent превышает 8 МиБ")
                    }
                    connection.inputStream.use { input ->
                        target.outputStream().use { output ->
                            val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
                            var total = 0L
                            while (true) {
                                val count = input.read(buffer)
                                if (count < 0) break
                                total += count
                                if (total > TorrentQueueStore.MAX_TORRENT_BYTES) {
                                    throw IOException("Файл .torrent превышает 8 МиБ")
                                }
                                output.write(buffer, 0, count)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return target
                } finally {
                    connection.disconnect()
                }
            }
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            target.delete()
            val safeMessage = IndexerErrorSanitizer.sanitize(
                error,
                listOfNotNull(
                    sourceConfig?.apiKey,
                    sourceCookie,
                    sourceConfig?.username,
                    sourceConfig?.password
                )
            )
            throw IOException(safeMessage)
        }
    }

    private fun effectivePort(uri: Uri): Int =
        if (uri.port >= 0) uri.port else if (uri.scheme.equals("https", true)) 443 else 80

    @android.annotation.TargetApi(35)
    override fun onTimeout(startId: Int, fgsType: Int) {
        // Only the dataSync mode is time-limited. Active viewing uses mediaPlayback mode.
        if (playbackActive.get()) {
            startForegroundFor(mediaPlayback = true, status = "Воспроизведение продолжается")
            return
        }
        serviceScope.launch {
            commandMutex.withLock {
                val saved = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { TorrentEngine.stop() }
                if (!saved) {
                    getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).notify(
                        NOTIFICATION_ID,
                        notification("Не все данные загрузок успели сохраниться. При следующем запуске будет выполнена проверка.", null)
                    )
                }
            }
            stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
            stopSelf(startId)
        }
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        streamServer?.close()
        streamServer = null
        if (playbackActive.get()) TorrentPlaybackBus.publish(null)
        serviceScope.cancel()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    private fun createChannel() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val channel = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "Jacket • загрузки и просмотр", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(channel)
        }
    }

    private fun notification(text: String, torrentId: String?): Notification {
        val pauseIntent = torrentId?.let { id ->
            PendingIntent.getService(
                this,
                id.hashCode(),
                Intent(this, TorrentDownloadService::class.java)
                    .setAction(ACTION_PAUSE_SELECTED)
                    .putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id),
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
            )
        }
        val openIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            this,
            4102,
            Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )
        val builder = NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
            .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_jacket)
            .setContentTitle("Jacket")
            .setContentText(text)
            .setContentIntent(openIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
            .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_SERVICE)
        if (pauseIntent != null) builder.addAction(
            R.drawable.ic_jacket,
            if (TorrentEngine.snapshots.value.firstOrNull { it.id == torrentId }?.paused == true) "Продолжить" else "Пауза",
            pauseIntent
        )
        return builder.build()
    }

    private fun formatRate(bytesPerSecond: Long): String = when {
        bytesPerSecond >= 1024L * 1024L -> "%.1f МБ/с".format(bytesPerSecond / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
        bytesPerSecond >= 1024L -> "%.0f КБ/с".format(bytesPerSecond / 1024.0)
        else -> "$bytesPerSecond Б/с"
    }

    companion object {
        const val EXTRA_MAGNET = "magnet"
        const val EXTRA_TITLE = "title"
        const val EXTRA_ID = "torrent_id"
        const val EXTRA_FILE_INDEX = "file_index"
        const val EXTRA_TORRENT_PATH = "torrent_path"
        const val EXTRA_TORRENT_URL = "torrent_url"
        const val EXTRA_INDEXER_ID = "indexer_id"
        const val EXTRA_DELETE_FILES = "delete_files"

        private const val ACTION_RESTORE = "app.jacket.action.RESTORE"
        private const val ACTION_ADD = "app.jacket.action.ADD"
        private const val ACTION_ADD_TORRENT = "app.jacket.action.ADD_TORRENT"
        private const val ACTION_ADD_TORRENT_URL = "app.jacket.action.ADD_TORRENT_URL"
        private const val ACTION_PAUSE = "app.jacket.action.PAUSE"
        private const val ACTION_RESUME = "app.jacket.action.RESUME"
        private const val ACTION_SELECT_FILE = "app.jacket.action.SELECT_FILE"
        private const val ACTION_REMOVE = "app.jacket.action.REMOVE"
        private const val ACTION_PLAY = "app.jacket.action.PLAY"
        private const val ACTION_STOP_PLAYBACK = "app.jacket.action.STOP_PLAYBACK"
        private const val ACTION_PAUSE_SELECTED = "app.jacket.action.PAUSE_SELECTED"
        private const val CHANNEL_ID = "jacket_downloads"
        private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 4101
        private const val POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 1_000L
        private const val RESUME_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000L
        private const val MAX_TORRENT_REDIRECTS = 3
        private val REDIRECT_CODES = setOf(301, 302, 303, 307, 308)

        private fun send(context: Context, action: String, extras: Intent.() -> Unit = {}) {
            val intent = Intent(context, TorrentDownloadService::class.java).setAction(action).apply(extras)
            ContextCompat.startForegroundService(context, intent)
        }

        fun restore(context: Context) = send(context, ACTION_RESTORE)
        fun add(context: Context, magnet: String, title: String) = send(context, ACTION_ADD) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_MAGNET, magnet)
            putExtra(EXTRA_TITLE, title)
        }
        fun addTorrentFile(context: Context, path: String, title: String? = null) = send(context, ACTION_ADD_TORRENT) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_TORRENT_PATH, path)
            putExtra(EXTRA_TITLE, title)
        }
        fun addTorrentUrl(context: Context, url: String, title: String, indexerId: String? = null) = send(context, ACTION_ADD_TORRENT_URL) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_TORRENT_URL, url)
            putExtra(EXTRA_TITLE, title)
            putExtra(EXTRA_INDEXER_ID, indexerId)
        }
        fun pause(context: Context, id: String) = send(context, ACTION_PAUSE) { putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id) }
        fun resume(context: Context, id: String) = send(context, ACTION_RESUME) { putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id) }
        fun selectFile(context: Context, id: String, fileIndex: Int) = send(context, ACTION_SELECT_FILE) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id)
            putExtra(EXTRA_FILE_INDEX, fileIndex)
        }
        fun remove(context: Context, id: String, deleteFiles: Boolean = false) = send(context, ACTION_REMOVE) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id)
            putExtra(EXTRA_DELETE_FILES, deleteFiles)
        }
        fun play(context: Context, id: String, fileIndex: Int) = send(context, ACTION_PLAY) {
            putExtra(EXTRA_ID, id)
            putExtra(EXTRA_FILE_INDEX, fileIndex)
        }
        fun stopPlayback(context: Context) = send(context, ACTION_STOP_PLAYBACK)
    }
}

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.util.AtomicFile
import kotlinx.coroutines.CompletableDeferred
import kotlinx.coroutines.awaitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import org.libtorrent4j.AlertListener
import org.libtorrent4j.AddTorrentParams
import org.libtorrent4j.Priority
import org.libtorrent4j.SessionManager
import org.libtorrent4j.SettingsPack
import org.libtorrent4j.TorrentFlags
import org.libtorrent4j.TorrentHandle
import org.libtorrent4j.TorrentInfo
import org.libtorrent4j.Vectors
import org.libtorrent4j.alerts.Alert
import org.libtorrent4j.alerts.AlertType
import org.libtorrent4j.alerts.MetadataReceivedAlert
import org.libtorrent4j.alerts.SaveResumeDataAlert
import org.libtorrent4j.swig.error_code
import org.libtorrent4j.swig.libtorrent
import org.libtorrent4j.swig.torrent_flags_t
import java.io.File
import java.io.RandomAccessFile
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap

/** Immutable file metadata suitable for UI; contains no native handles. */
data class TorrentFileSnapshot(
    val index: Int,
    val path: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val downloadedBytes: Long,
    val progress: Float,
    val priority: Int,
    val isSelected: Boolean
)

/** Immutable UI snapshot; native handles never escape the engine. */
data class TorrentSnapshot(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val progress: Float,
    val downloadedBytes: Long,
    val totalBytes: Long,
    val downloadBytesPerSecond: Int,
    val uploadBytesPerSecond: Int,
    val seeds: Int,
    val peers: Int,
    val state: String,
    val paused: Boolean,
    val files: List<TorrentFileSnapshot> = emptyList(),
    val selectedFileIndex: Int? = null,
    val selectedFileProgress: Float = 0f,
    val error: String? = null,
    val localStreamPort: Int? = null
)

data class TorrentStreamTarget(
    val id: String,
    val fileIndex: Int,
    val name: String,
    val sizeBytes: Long,
    val filePath: String,
    val progress: Float
)

/** Owns the single libtorrent session. JNI calls are kept on the service IO dispatcher. */
object TorrentEngine {
    @Volatile private var manager: SessionManager? = null
    @Volatile private var appContext: Context? = null
    @Volatile private var alertListener: AlertListener? = null

    private data class ActiveStreamWindow(val fileIndex: Int, val firstPiece: Int)

    private val errors = ConcurrentHashMap<String, String>()
    private val pendingResumeSaves = ConcurrentHashMap<String, CompletableDeferred<Boolean>>()
    private val activeStreamWindows = ConcurrentHashMap<String, ActiveStreamWindow>()
    @Volatile private var localStreamPort: Int? = null
    private val _snapshots = MutableStateFlow<List<TorrentSnapshot>>(emptyList())
    val snapshots: StateFlow<List<TorrentSnapshot>> = _snapshots.asStateFlow()

    @Synchronized
    fun start(context: Context) {
        appContext = context.applicationContext
        if (manager?.isRunning == true) {
            restorePending(context)
            return
        }

        val instance = SessionManager()
        val listener = object : AlertListener {
            override fun types(): IntArray = intArrayOf(
                AlertType.SAVE_RESUME_DATA.swig(),
                AlertType.SAVE_RESUME_DATA_FAILED.swig(),
                AlertType.METADATA_RECEIVED.swig(),
                AlertType.TORRENT_ERROR.swig(),
                AlertType.FILE_ERROR.swig(),
                AlertType.HASH_FAILED.swig()
            )

            override fun alert(alert: Alert<*>) {
                when (alert.type()) {
                    AlertType.SAVE_RESUME_DATA -> saveResumeAlert(alert as SaveResumeDataAlert)
                    AlertType.SAVE_RESUME_DATA_FAILED -> recordAlertError(alert)
                    AlertType.METADATA_RECEIVED -> {
                        val received = alert as MetadataReceivedAlert
                        runCatching {
                            val id = received.handle().infoHash().toHex()
                            val selected = TorrentQueueStore(appContext!!).get(id)?.selectedFileIndex
                            if (selected != null) applyFileSelection(received.handle(), selected)
                        }.onFailure { recordErrorFromAlert(alert, it.message ?: "Не удалось прочитать метаданные") }
                    }
                    AlertType.TORRENT_ERROR, AlertType.FILE_ERROR, AlertType.HASH_FAILED -> recordAlertError(alert)
                    else -> Unit
                }
            }
        }
        alertListener = listener
        instance.addListener(listener)

        val (downloadLimit, uploadLimit) = rateLimits(context)
        val settings = SettingsPack.defaultSettings()
            .downloadRateLimit(downloadLimit)
            .uploadRateLimit(uploadLimit)
        instance.start(org.libtorrent4j.SessionParams(settings))
        instance.startDht()
        manager = instance

        restorePending(context)
    }

    @Synchronized
    private fun restoreItem(context: Context, item: QueuedTorrent) {
        val current = manager ?: return
        val resumeFile = resumeFile(context, item.id)
        val atomicResumeFile = AtomicFile(resumeFile)
        try {
            // openRead() is essential: it rolls back an interrupted AtomicFile write
            // from the last committed backup. Reading File.readBytes() directly
            // bypasses that recovery path and can discard a valid checkpoint.
            val storedResumeData = atomicResumeFile.openRead().use { input ->
                require(resumeFile.length() in 1..ResumeDataEnvelope.MAX_STORED_BYTES) {
                    "Resume data отсутствует или слишком велик"
                }
                input.readBytes()
            }
            val payload = ResumeDataEnvelope.decode(storedResumeData)
            val parseError = error_code()
            val raw = libtorrent.read_resume_data_ex(Vectors.bytes2byte_vector(payload), parseError)
            require(parseError.value() == 0) {
                "Не удалось прочитать fast-resume: ${parseError.message()}"
            }
            val restoredHash = raw.getInfo_hashes().get_best().to_hex().lowercase()
            require(restoredHash == item.id.lowercase()) {
                "Fast-resume относится к другой раздаче"
            }
            raw.setSave_path(downloadDirectory(context).absolutePath)
            if (TorrentQueueStore(context).isPaused(item.id)) {
                raw.setFlags(raw.getFlags().or_(TorrentFlags.PAUSED))
            }
            current.swig().async_add_torrent(raw)
            errors.remove(item.id)
            return
        } catch (_: java.io.FileNotFoundException) {
            // No checkpoint exists yet; the durable queue entry below is enough
            // to rebuild the torrent from its private metainfo or magnet URI.
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            // Delete through AtomicFile so a stale .bak cannot resurrect a
            // checkpoint already rejected by its checksum, parser or info-hash.
            atomicResumeFile.delete()
            errors[item.id] = "Сохранённое состояние повреждено. Проверяем раздачу заново."
        }

        val torrentInfo = item.torrentPath
            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?.let { path -> runCatching { File(path).takeIf(File::isFile)?.let(::TorrentInfo) }.getOrNull() }
            ?.takeIf { it.isValid }
        when {
            torrentInfo != null -> current.download(
                torrentInfo, downloadDirectory(context), null, null, null, torrent_flags_t()
            )
            !item.magnetUri.isNullOrBlank() -> current.download(
                item.magnetUri, downloadDirectory(context), torrent_flags_t()
            )
            else -> throw IllegalStateException("Не удалось восстановить раздачу: нет корректного .torrent или magnet")
        }
        if (TorrentQueueStore(context).isPaused(item.id)) findHandle(item.id)?.let(::pauseHandle)
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun add(context: Context, magnet: String, title: String) {
        start(context)
        val queued = TorrentQueueStore(context).add(magnet, title)
        if (findHandle(queued.id) == null && !wifiOnlyRestricted(context)) {
            manager?.download(queued.magnetUri!!, downloadDirectory(context), torrent_flags_t())
        }
        refresh()
    }

    /** Copies the provided torrent metadata into app-private storage before queueing it. */
    @Synchronized
    fun addTorrent(context: Context, source: File, title: String? = null): QueuedTorrent {
        require(source.isFile && source.length() in 1..TorrentQueueStore.MAX_TORRENT_BYTES) {
            "Файл .torrent отсутствует или превышает 8 МиБ"
        }
        val info = TorrentInfo(source)
        require(info.isValid) { "Файл не является корректным .torrent" }
        val id = info.infoHash().toHex().lowercase()
        val directory = torrentMetainfoDirectory(context)
        directory.mkdirs()
        val target = File(directory, "$id.torrent")
        if (source.canonicalFile != target.canonicalFile) source.copyTo(target, overwrite = true)
        val queued = TorrentQueueStore(context).addTorrentFile(target, id, title ?: info.name())
        start(context)
        if (findHandle(id) == null && !wifiOnlyRestricted(context)) {
            manager?.download(info, downloadDirectory(context), null, null, null, torrent_flags_t())
        }
        refresh()
        return queued
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun selectFile(context: Context, id: String, fileIndex: Int) {
        val item = TorrentQueueStore(context).get(id) ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Загрузка не найдена")
        val handle = findHandle(item.id) ?: throw IllegalStateException("Торрент ещё подключается. Повторите через несколько секунд.")
        val info = handle.torrentFile() ?: throw IllegalStateException("Ожидаются метаданные раздачи")
        require(fileIndex in 0 until info.numFiles()) { "Неверный индекс файла" }
        require(!info.files().padFileAt(fileIndex)) { "Нельзя воспроизводить служебный pad-файл" }

        TorrentQueueStore(context).selectFile(id, fileIndex)
        applyFileSelection(handle, fileIndex)
        refresh()
    }

    @Synchronized
    private fun applyFileSelection(handle: TorrentHandle, fileIndex: Int) {
        require(handle.isValid) { "Торрент уже удалён" }
        val info = handle.torrentFile() ?: throw IllegalStateException("Метаданные раздачи ещё не получены")
        val files = info.files()
        require(fileIndex in 0 until files.numFiles())

        // Keep the selected file eligible, but let HTTP Range requests drive
        // the active piece window. Global sequential mode starts at piece zero
        // and is a poor fit for seeking/streaming.
        handle.clearPieceDeadlines()
        runCatching { activeStreamWindows.remove(handle.infoHash().toHex()) }
        handle.unsetFlags(TorrentFlags.SEQUENTIAL_DOWNLOAD)

        val priorities = Priority.array(Priority.IGNORE, files.numFiles())
        priorities[fileIndex] = Priority.TOP_PRIORITY
        handle.prioritizeFiles(priorities)
    }

    /**
     * Re-prioritizes a bounded forward window around the byte range requested
     * by ExoPlayer. The first not-yet-downloaded piece gets the nearest
     * deadline; following pieces are assigned increasing deadlines.
     */
    @Synchronized
    private fun prioritizeStreamRange(
        id: String,
        handle: TorrentHandle,
        storage: org.libtorrent4j.FileStorage,
        fileIndex: Int,
        start: Long,
        length: Long
    ) {
        val fileSize = storage.fileSize(fileIndex)
        val plan = StreamPiecePlanner.plan(
            fileOffset = storage.fileOffset(fileIndex),
            fileSize = fileSize,
            pieceLength = storage.pieceLength().toLong(),
            requestStart = start,
            requestLength = length,
            firstFilePiece = storage.pieceIndexAtFile(fileIndex),
            lastFilePiece = storage.lastPieceIndexAtFile(fileIndex)
        )
        val window = ActiveStreamWindow(fileIndex, plan.firstPiece)
        if (activeStreamWindows[id] == window) return

        handle.clearPieceDeadlines()
        plan.deadlines.forEach { deadline ->
            if (!handle.havePiece(deadline.pieceIndex)) {
                handle.setPieceDeadline(deadline.pieceIndex, deadline.deadlineMs)
            }
        }
        activeStreamWindows[id] = window
    }

    /** Clear playback-only deadlines when streaming stops or the task is paused. */
    @Synchronized
    fun clearStreamPriorities(id: String) {
        activeStreamWindows.remove(id)
        findHandle(id)?.let { handle ->
            runCatching { handle.clearPieceDeadlines() }
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun pause(context: Context, id: String) {
        clearStreamPriorities(id)
        TorrentQueueStore(context).setPaused(id, true)
        findHandle(id)?.let(::pauseHandle)
        findHandle(id)?.let(::requestResumeData)
        refresh()
    }

    private fun pauseHandle(handle: TorrentHandle) {
        handle.setFlags(TorrentFlags.AUTO_MANAGED.inv(), TorrentFlags.AUTO_MANAGED)
        handle.pause()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun resume(context: Context, id: String) {
        TorrentQueueStore(context).setPaused(id, false)
        val handle = findHandle(id)
        if (handle != null) {
            val selected = TorrentQueueStore(context).get(id)?.selectedFileIndex
            if (selected != null && handle.torrentFile() != null) applyFileSelection(handle, selected)
            handle.setFlags(TorrentFlags.AUTO_MANAGED, TorrentFlags.AUTO_MANAGED)
            handle.resume()
        } else {
            restorePending(context)
        }
        refresh()
    }

    /** Removes only the task from the queue. The downloaded data is preserved. */
    @Synchronized
    fun remove(context: Context, id: String, deleteFiles: Boolean = false) {
        val item = TorrentQueueStore(context).get(id)
        val handle = findHandle(id)
        // Capture metadata while the handle is still valid; this works for magnet and .torrent tasks.
        val info = handle?.let { runCatching { it.torrentFile() }.getOrNull() }
            ?: item?.torrentPath?.let { runCatching { TorrentInfo(File(it)) }.getOrNull() }
        val base = downloadDirectory(context).canonicalFile
        if (deleteFiles && info != null) {
            val storage = info.files()
            for (i in 0 until storage.numFiles()) {
                if (storage.padFileAt(i)) continue
                val path = File(storage.filePath(i, base.absolutePath)).canonicalFile
                if (path.toPath().startsWith(base.toPath()) && path.isFile) path.delete()
            }
        }
        clearStreamPriorities(id)
        handle?.let { manager?.remove(it) }
        TorrentQueueStore(context).remove(id)
        AtomicFile(resumeFile(context, id)).delete()
        refresh()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun setRateLimits(context: Context, downloadKbps: Int, uploadKbps: Int) {
        val down = downloadKbps.coerceAtLeast(0)
        val up = uploadKbps.coerceAtLeast(0)
        context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(RATE_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
            .putInt(KEY_DOWNLOAD_KBPS, down).putInt(KEY_UPLOAD_KBPS, up).apply()
        manager?.applySettings(SettingsPack().downloadRateLimit(kbpsToBytes(down)).uploadRateLimit(kbpsToBytes(up)))
    }

    fun rateLimits(context: Context): Pair<Int, Int> {
        val prefs = context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(RATE_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        return prefs.getInt(KEY_DOWNLOAD_KBPS, 0) to prefs.getInt(KEY_UPLOAD_KBPS, 0)
    }

    fun isWifiOnly(context: Context): Boolean =
        context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(RATE_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getBoolean(KEY_WIFI_ONLY, false)

    @Synchronized
    fun setWifiOnly(context: Context, enabled: Boolean) {
        context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(RATE_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
            .putBoolean(KEY_WIFI_ONLY, enabled).apply()
        // A service poll will start any queued tasks when Wi-Fi becomes available.
    }

    /** Resumes persisted queue entries that do not yet have a live native handle. */
    @Synchronized
    fun restorePending(context: Context) {
        if (manager == null || wifiOnlyRestricted(context)) {
            refresh()
            return
        }
        val store = TorrentQueueStore(context)
        store.all().forEach { item ->
            if (findHandle(item.id) == null) {
                runCatching { restoreItem(context, item) }
                    .onFailure { errors[item.id] = it.message ?: "Не удалось восстановить загрузку" }
            }
        }
        refresh()
    }

    private fun wifiOnlyRestricted(context: Context): Boolean {
        if (!isWifiOnly(context)) return false
        val connectivity = context.applicationContext.getSystemService(ConnectivityManager::class.java)
            ?: return true
        val network = connectivity.activeNetwork ?: return true
        val capabilities = connectivity.getNetworkCapabilities(network) ?: return true
        return !capabilities.hasTransport(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI)
    }


    private fun kbpsToBytes(value: Int): Int = if (value <= 0) 0 else (value.toLong() * 1024L).coerceAtMost(Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt()

    fun reportError(id: String, message: String) {
        val safeId = id.takeIf { it.matches(Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")) } ?: "unknown"
        errors[safeId] = message.replace(Regex("[\\r\\n\\t]+"), " ").take(300)
        refresh()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun setLocalStreamPort(port: Int?) {
        localStreamPort = port?.takeIf { it in 8090..8099 }
        refresh()
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun refresh() {
        val current = manager ?: run {
            _snapshots.value = emptyList()
            return
        }
        val context = appContext ?: return
        val store = TorrentQueueStore(context)
        val pausedIds = store.pausedIds()
        val vector = current.swig().get_torrents()
        val rows = ArrayList<TorrentSnapshot>(vector.size)

        for (index in 0 until vector.size) {
            val handle = TorrentHandle(vector.get(index))
            if (!handle.isValid) continue
            try {
                val status = handle.status()
                val id = handle.infoHash().toHex()
                val queued = store.get(id)
                val paused = id in pausedIds
                if (paused) pauseHandle(handle)
                val info = handle.torrentFile()
                val storage = info?.files()
                val progress = if (info != null) handle.fileProgress() else longArrayOf()
                var priorities = if (info != null) handle.filePriorities() else emptyArray()
                // A resume-data restore already includes metadata and will not emit METADATA_RECEIVED.
                val selectedIndex = queued?.selectedFileIndex
                if (info != null && selectedIndex != null &&
                    selectedIndex in priorities.indices &&
                    priorities[selectedIndex] != Priority.TOP_PRIORITY
                ) {
                    runCatching {
                        applyFileSelection(handle, selectedIndex)
                        priorities = handle.filePriorities()
                    }.onFailure { errors[id] = it.message ?: "Не удалось восстановить приоритет файла" }
                }
                val files = if (storage != null) {
                    (0 until storage.numFiles()).map { fileIndex ->
                        val size = storage.fileSize(fileIndex).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                        val downloaded = progress.getOrNull(fileIndex)?.coerceIn(0L, size) ?: 0L
                        TorrentFileSnapshot(
                            index = fileIndex,
                            path = storage.filePath(fileIndex),
                            sizeBytes = size,
                            downloadedBytes = downloaded,
                            progress = if (size == 0L) 0f else downloaded.toFloat() / size.toFloat(),
                            priority = priorities.getOrNull(fileIndex)?.swig()?.toInt() ?: 0,
                            isSelected = queued?.selectedFileIndex == fileIndex
                        )
                    }.filterNot { runCatching { storage.padFileAt(it.index) }.getOrDefault(true) }
                } else emptyList()
                val selectedProgress = files.firstOrNull { it.index == selectedIndex }?.progress ?: 0f
                rows += TorrentSnapshot(
                    id = id,
                    name = status.name().ifBlank { queued?.title ?: "Торрент ${id.take(10)}" },
                    progress = status.progress().coerceIn(0f, 1f),
                    downloadedBytes = status.totalWantedDone().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    totalBytes = status.totalWanted().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    downloadBytesPerSecond = status.downloadPayloadRate().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    uploadBytesPerSecond = status.uploadPayloadRate().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    seeds = status.numSeeds().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    peers = status.numPeers().coerceAtLeast(0),
                    state = status.state().name,
                    paused = paused,
                    files = files,
                    selectedFileIndex = selectedIndex,
                    selectedFileProgress = selectedProgress,
                    error = errors[id],
                    localStreamPort = localStreamPort
                )
            } catch (error: Exception) {
                errors[runCatching { handle.infoHash().toHex() }.getOrDefault("unknown")] =
                    error.message ?: "Ошибка чтения состояния торрента"
            }
        }
        val activeIds = rows.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id }
        store.all().filterNot { it.id in activeIds }.forEach { queued ->
            rows += TorrentSnapshot(
                id = queued.id,
                name = queued.title,
                progress = 0f,
                downloadedBytes = 0L,
                totalBytes = 0L,
                downloadBytesPerSecond = 0,
                uploadBytesPerSecond = 0,
                seeds = 0,
                peers = 0,
                state = if (wifiOnlyRestricted(context)) "WAITING_FOR_WIFI" else "QUEUED",
                paused = store.isPaused(queued.id),
                error = errors[queued.id],
                localStreamPort = localStreamPort
            )
        }
        _snapshots.value = rows.sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    }

    /** Wait until every piece intersecting the requested file byte range is complete on disk. */
    fun awaitRange(id: String, fileIndex: Int, start: Long, length: Long, timeoutPerPieceMs: Long = 25_000L): File {
        require(start >= 0L && length > 0L)
        val handle = findHandle(id) ?: throw IllegalStateException("Торрент не найден")
        require(handle.isValid) { "Торрент больше не активен" }
        val info = handle.torrentFile() ?: throw IllegalStateException("Ожидаются метаданные торрента")
        val storage = info.files()
        require(fileIndex in 0 until storage.numFiles()) { "Неверный индекс файла" }
        val fileSize = storage.fileSize(fileIndex)
        require(start < fileSize && length <= fileSize - start) { "Запрошенный диапазон выходит за пределы файла" }
        val selected = TorrentQueueStore(appContext!!).get(id)?.selectedFileIndex
        if (selected != fileIndex) {
            val completedBytes = handle.fileProgress().getOrNull(fileIndex) ?: 0L
            if (completedBytes < fileSize) {
                throw IllegalStateException("Дополнительный файл не загружен полностью")
            }
            // Fully downloaded subtitle sidecars can be served without switching priorities.
        }
        val absoluteStart = Math.addExact(storage.fileOffset(fileIndex), start)
        require(length - 1L <= Long.MAX_VALUE - absoluteStart) { "Диапазон слишком большой" }
        val absoluteEnd = absoluteStart + length - 1L
        val pieceLength = storage.pieceLength().toLong()
        val firstPiece = (absoluteStart / pieceLength).toInt()
        val lastPiece = (absoluteEnd / pieceLength).toInt()

        if (selected == fileIndex) {
            prioritizeStreamRange(id, handle, storage, fileIndex, start, length)
        }

        for (piece in firstPiece..lastPiece) {
            val began = System.currentTimeMillis()
            while (!handle.havePiece(piece)) {
                if (TorrentQueueStore(appContext!!).isPaused(id)) {
                    throw IllegalStateException("Загрузка приостановлена")
                }
                if (System.currentTimeMillis() - began > timeoutPerPieceMs) {
                    throw java.net.SocketTimeoutException("Ожидание части торрента превысило 25 секунд")
                }
                Thread.sleep(120)
            }
        }
        val root = File(handle.savePath()).canonicalFile
        val file = File(storage.filePath(fileIndex, root.absolutePath)).canonicalFile
        require(file.toPath().startsWith(root.toPath())) { "Путь файла выходит за каталог загрузки" }
        require(file.isFile && file.length() >= start && length <= file.length() - start) { "Файл ещё не записан на диск" }
        return file
    }

    fun target(id: String, fileIndex: Int): TorrentStreamTarget {
        val handle = findHandle(id) ?: throw IllegalStateException("Торрент не найден")
        val info = handle.torrentFile() ?: throw IllegalStateException("Метаданные ещё не получены")
        val storage = info.files()
        require(fileIndex in 0 until storage.numFiles())
        val size = storage.fileSize(fileIndex)
        val progress = handle.fileProgress().getOrNull(fileIndex) ?: 0L
        return TorrentStreamTarget(
            id = id,
            fileIndex = fileIndex,
            name = storage.fileName(fileIndex),
            sizeBytes = size,
            filePath = storage.filePath(fileIndex),
            progress = if (size <= 0L) 0f else (progress.toFloat() / size.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        )
    }

    /** Returns matching subtitle tracks already present in the torrent metadata. */
    fun subtitleTargets(id: String, videoFileIndex: Int): List<TorrentStreamTarget> {
        val handle = findHandle(id) ?: return emptyList()
        val info = handle.torrentFile() ?: return emptyList()
        val storage = info.files()
        if (videoFileIndex !in 0 until storage.numFiles()) return emptyList()
        val videoName = storage.fileName(videoFileIndex)
        val baseName = videoName.substringAfterLast('/').substringBeforeLast('.').lowercase()
        val fileProgress = handle.fileProgress()
        return (0 until storage.numFiles()).mapNotNull { index ->
            if (index == videoFileIndex || storage.padFileAt(index)) return@mapNotNull null
            val name = storage.fileName(index)
            val extension = name.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
            if (extension !in setOf("srt", "vtt")) return@mapNotNull null
            val candidateBase = name.substringAfterLast('/').substringBeforeLast('.').lowercase()
            if (candidateBase != baseName && !candidateBase.startsWith("$baseName.")) return@mapNotNull null
            val size = storage.fileSize(index)
            // Never change selected-file priorities while ExoPlayer requests subtitle bytes.
            // A sidecar is exposed only when it is already fully available on disk.
            if (size <= 0L || fileProgress.getOrNull(index) != size) return@mapNotNull null
            target(id, index)
        }.take(MAX_SUBTITLE_TRACKS)
    }

    /** Wait for native save-resume alerts before destroying libtorrent's session. */
    suspend fun stop(timeoutMs: Long = RESUME_SAVE_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS): Boolean {
        val current = manager ?: return true
        val saved = saveAllResumeDataAndAwait(timeoutMs)
        current.removeListener(alertListener)
        current.stop()
        manager = null
        alertListener = null
        localStreamPort = null
        _snapshots.value = emptyList()
        return saved
    }

    fun saveAllResumeData() {
        val current = manager ?: return
        val vector = current.swig().get_torrents()
        for (index in 0 until vector.size) {
            val handle = TorrentHandle(vector.get(index))
            if (handle.isValid) requestResumeData(handle)
        }
    }

    suspend fun saveAllResumeDataAndAwait(timeoutMs: Long = RESUME_SAVE_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS): Boolean {
        val current = manager ?: return true
        val vector = current.swig().get_torrents()
        val requests = ArrayList<Pair<String, CompletableDeferred<Boolean>>>(vector.size)
        for (index in 0 until vector.size) {
            val handle = TorrentHandle(vector.get(index))
            if (handle.isValid) {
                val id = handle.infoHash().toHex().lowercase()
                requests += id to requestResumeData(handle)
            }
        }
        if (requests.isEmpty()) return true
        val results = withTimeoutOrNull(timeoutMs.coerceAtLeast(1L)) {
            requests.map { it.second }.awaitAll()
        }
        if (results == null) {
            requests.forEach { (id, ticket) ->
                if (!ticket.isCompleted && pendingResumeSaves.remove(id, ticket)) ticket.cancel()
            }
            return false
        }
        return results.all { it }
    }

    private fun requestResumeData(handle: TorrentHandle): CompletableDeferred<Boolean> {
        val id = handle.infoHash().toHex().lowercase()
        val ticket = CompletableDeferred<Boolean>()
        val active = pendingResumeSaves.putIfAbsent(id, ticket)
        if (active != null) return active
        runCatching { handle.saveResumeData(TorrentHandle.SAVE_INFO_DICT) }
            .onFailure { error ->
                pendingResumeSaves.remove(id, ticket)
                errors[id] = friendlyError(error.message ?: "Не удалось запросить сохранение состояния")
                ticket.complete(false)
            }
        return ticket
    }

    @Synchronized
    private fun saveResumeAlert(alert: SaveResumeDataAlert) {
        runCatching {
            val id = alert.handle().infoHash().toHex().lowercase()
            val context = appContext ?: return
            val file = resumeFile(context, id)
            file.parentFile?.mkdirs()
            val bytes = ResumeDataEnvelope.encode(AddTorrentParams.writeResumeDataBuf(alert.params()))
            val atomic = AtomicFile(file)
            val output = atomic.startWrite()
            try {
                output.write(bytes)
                atomic.finishWrite(output)
            } catch (error: Exception) {
                atomic.failWrite(output)
                throw error
            }
            errors.remove(id)
            pendingResumeSaves.remove(id)?.complete(true)
        }.onFailure {
            val id = runCatching { alert.handle().infoHash().toHex().lowercase() }.getOrNull()
            id?.let { hash -> pendingResumeSaves.remove(hash)?.complete(false) }
            recordErrorFromAlert(alert, it.message ?: "Не удалось записать resume data")
        }
    }

    private fun recordAlertError(alert: Alert<*>) {
        val id = runCatching {
            (alert as? org.libtorrent4j.alerts.TorrentAlert<*>)?.handle()?.infoHash()?.toHex()?.lowercase()
        }.getOrNull()
        id?.let { pendingResumeSaves.remove(it)?.complete(false) }
        recordErrorFromAlert(alert, alert.message().take(500))
    }

    private fun recordErrorFromAlert(alert: Alert<*>, message: String) {
        val id = runCatching {
            (alert as? org.libtorrent4j.alerts.TorrentAlert<*>)?.handle()?.infoHash()?.toHex()
        }.getOrNull() ?: "unknown"
        errors[id] = friendlyError(message.ifBlank { "Ошибка торрент-движка" })
    }

    private fun friendlyError(message: String): String {
        val clean = message.replace(Regex("[\\r\\n\\t]+"), " ").take(240)
        val lower = clean.lowercase()
        return when {
            "no space" in lower || "disk" in lower || "write" in lower || "permission" in lower ->
                "Ошибка диска или записи: $clean"
            "timeout" in lower || "timed out" in lower ->
                "Истекло время ожидания соединения: $clean"
            "hash" in lower || "corrupt" in lower || "invalid resume" in lower ->
                "Проверка целостности не пройдена: $clean"
            "connection" in lower || "socket" in lower || "network" in lower ->
                "Потеряно сетевое соединение: $clean"
            "no peers" in lower || "no seed" in lower ->
                "Пока нет доступных пиров или сидов: $clean"
            else -> clean
        }
    }

    private fun findHandle(id: String): TorrentHandle? {
        val current = manager ?: return null
        val vector = current.swig().get_torrents()
        for (index in 0 until vector.size) {
            val handle = TorrentHandle(vector.get(index))
            if (runCatching { handle.isValid && handle.infoHash().toHex().equals(id, true) }.getOrDefault(false)) {
                return handle
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    fun resumeFile(context: Context, id: String): File = File(File(context.filesDir, "torrent-resume"), "$id.fastresume")
    private fun torrentMetainfoDirectory(context: Context): File = File(context.filesDir, "torrent-metainfo").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun downloadDirectory(context: Context): File =
        File(context.getExternalFilesDir(null) ?: context.filesDir, "Downloads").apply { mkdirs() }

    private const val RATE_PREFS = "jacket_torrent_settings"
    private const val RESUME_SAVE_SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS = 12_000L
    private const val KEY_DOWNLOAD_KBPS = "download_kbps"
    private const val KEY_UPLOAD_KBPS = "upload_kbps"
    private const val KEY_WIFI_ONLY = "wifi_only"
    private const val MAX_SUBTITLE_TRACKS = 12
}

/** Versioned CRC envelope. Legacy raw libtorrent fast-resume files remain supported. */
internal object ResumeDataEnvelope {
    private val MAGIC = "JACKETR1".toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII)
    private const val HEADER_BYTES = 16
    private const val MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024
    internal const val MAX_STORED_BYTES = MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES + HEADER_BYTES

    fun encode(payload: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        require(payload.isNotEmpty() && payload.size <= MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES) { "Недопустимый размер resume data" }
        val checksum = java.util.zip.CRC32().apply { update(payload) }.value
        return java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(HEADER_BYTES + payload.size)
            .order(java.nio.ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN)
            .put(MAGIC)
            .putInt(payload.size)
            .putInt(checksum.toInt())
            .put(payload)
            .array()
    }

    fun decode(stored: ByteArray): ByteArray {
        require(stored.isNotEmpty() && stored.size <= MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES + HEADER_BYTES) {
            "Resume data отсутствует или слишком велик"
        }
        if (stored.size < MAGIC.size || !stored.copyOfRange(0, MAGIC.size).contentEquals(MAGIC)) return stored
        require(stored.size >= HEADER_BYTES) { "Обрезан заголовок resume data" }
        val buffer = java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(stored).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN)
        buffer.position(MAGIC.size)
        val size = buffer.int
        val checksum = buffer.int.toLong() and 0xffffffffL
        require(size in 1..MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES && buffer.remaining() == size) { "Некорректный размер resume data" }
        val payload = ByteArray(size)
        buffer.get(payload)
        val actual = java.util.zip.CRC32().apply { update(payload) }.value
        require(actual == checksum) { "Контрольная сумма resume data не совпадает" }
        return payload
    }
}

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow

data class PlaybackSubtitle(
    val url: String,
    val mimeType: String,
    val language: String?,
    val label: String
)

/** Process-local hand-off from the foreground download service to the Compose player. */
data class StreamPlaybackRequest(
    val title: String,
    val url: String,
    val progressKey: String,
    val initialPositionMs: Long,
    val subtitles: List<PlaybackSubtitle> = emptyList()
)

object TorrentPlaybackBus {
    private val _active = MutableStateFlow<StreamPlaybackRequest?>(null)
    val active: StateFlow<StreamPlaybackRequest?> = _active.asStateFlow()

    fun publish(request: StreamPlaybackRequest?) {
        _active.value = request
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/TorrentQueueCodec.kt

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import java.io.File
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.Base64

/** Pure queue persistence codec, intentionally independent from Android and libtorrent JNI. */
object TorrentQueueCodec {
    private const val HEADER = "JACKET_QUEUE_V2"
    private const val SEPARATOR = "\t"
    private val QUEUE_ID = Regex("(?i)[a-f0-9]{24,64}")
    private val LEGACY_FILE_ID = Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")

    fun encode(items: List<QueuedTorrent>): String {
        require(items.size <= MAX_QUEUE_ITEMS) { "В очереди слишком много загрузок" }
        items.forEach { item ->
            require(item.id.matches(QUEUE_ID)) { "Некорректный ID очереди" }
            require(item.title.length <= MAX_TITLE_LENGTH) { "Название загрузки слишком длинное" }
            require(item.magnetUri == null || (
                item.magnetUri.startsWith("magnet:?") && item.magnetUri.length <= MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH
            )) { "Некорректная magnet-ссылка" }
            require(item.torrentPath == null || item.torrentPath.length <= MAX_PATH_LENGTH) {
                "Путь к .torrent слишком длинный"
            }
            require(item.selectedFileIndex == null || item.selectedFileIndex >= 0) {
                "Индекс выбранного файла не может быть отрицательным"
            }
        }
        val estimatedBytes = HEADER.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).size.toLong() +
            items.sumOf(::encodedRecordBytes)
        require(estimatedBytes <= MAX_ENCODED_BYTES) { "Файл очереди слишком большой" }

        return buildString {
            append(HEADER)
            items.forEach { item ->
                append('\n')
                append(item.id).append(SEPARATOR)
                append(encodeField(item.title)).append(SEPARATOR)
                append(encodeField(item.magnetUri)).append(SEPARATOR)
                append(encodeField(item.torrentPath)).append(SEPARATOR)
                append(item.selectedFileIndex ?: -1)
            }
        }
    }

    fun decode(value: String): List<QueuedTorrent> {
        require(value.length <= MAX_ENCODED_BYTES) { "Файл очереди слишком большой" }
        val lines = value.lineSequence().toList()
        require(lines.firstOrNull() == HEADER) { "Неизвестный формат очереди" }
        return lines.drop(1).filter(String::isNotBlank).take(MAX_QUEUE_ITEMS).mapNotNull { line ->
            runCatching {
                val fields = line.split(SEPARATOR)
                require(fields.size == 5) { "Повреждена запись очереди" }
                val id = fields[0]
                require(id.matches(QUEUE_ID)) { "Некорректный ID очереди" }
                val title = decodeField(fields[1]).orEmpty()
                require(title.length <= MAX_TITLE_LENGTH) { "Название загрузки слишком длинное" }
                val magnet = decodeField(fields[2])
                require(magnet == null || (magnet.startsWith("magnet:?") && magnet.length <= MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH)) {
                    "Некорректная magnet-ссылка"
                }
                val path = decodeField(fields[3])
                require(path == null || path.length <= MAX_PATH_LENGTH) { "Путь к .torrent слишком длинный" }
                val index = fields[4].toIntOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= 0 }
                QueuedTorrent(
                    id = id,
                    title = title,
                    magnetUri = magnet,
                    torrentPath = path,
                    selectedFileIndex = index
                )
            }.getOrNull()
        }.distinctBy { it.id.lowercase() }
    }

    /**
     * Migrates the former JSON SharedPreferences queue into the current model.
     * Magnet hashes are derived from their URI instead of trusting a stale ID.
     * File-only entries retain a valid infohash or use a hash-named metainfo file.
     */
    internal fun decodeLegacy(value: String): List<QueuedTorrent> {
        require(value.length <= MAX_ENCODED_BYTES) { "Файл очереди слишком большой" }
        val array = runCatching { JSONArray(value) }
            .getOrElse { throw IllegalArgumentException("Повреждена старая очередь торрентов", it) }
        val migrated = ArrayList<QueuedTorrent>()
        val seen = HashSet<String>()
        var encodedBytes = HEADER.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).size.toLong()
        for (index in 0 until minOf(array.length(), MAX_QUEUE_ITEMS)) {
            val item = array.optJSONObject(index) ?: continue
            val magnet = item.optString("magnet").takeIf {
                it.startsWith("magnet:?") && it.length <= MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH
            }
            val path = item.optString("torrentPath").takeIf { rawPath ->
                rawPath.length <= MAX_PATH_LENGTH && rawPath.isNotBlank() &&
                    File(rawPath).let { file -> file.isFile && file.length() in 1..MAX_TORRENT_BYTES }
            }
            if (magnet == null && path == null) continue
            val id = magnet?.let { TorrentQueueStore.idFor(it) }
                ?: item.optString("id").lowercase().takeIf { it.matches(LEGACY_FILE_ID) }
                ?: path?.let { runCatching { TorrentQueueStore.idForFile(File(it)) }.getOrNull() }
                ?: continue
            if (!seen.add(id.lowercase())) continue
            val migratedItem = QueuedTorrent(
                id = id,
                title = item.optString("title").trim().take(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH)
                    .ifBlank { "Торрент ${id.take(10)}" },
                magnetUri = magnet,
                torrentPath = path,
                selectedFileIndex = item.optInt("selectedFileIndex", -1).takeIf { it >= 0 }
            )
            val recordBytes = encodedRecordBytes(migratedItem)
            if (encodedBytes + recordBytes > MAX_ENCODED_BYTES) {
                seen.remove(id.lowercase())
                continue
            }
            migrated += migratedItem
            encodedBytes += recordBytes
        }
        return migrated
    }

    private fun encodedRecordBytes(item: QueuedTorrent): Long =
        item.id.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).size.toLong() + 4L +
            encodedFieldBytes(item.title) + encodedFieldBytes(item.magnetUri) +
            encodedFieldBytes(item.torrentPath) +
            (item.selectedFileIndex ?: -1).toString().length + 1L

    private fun encodedFieldBytes(value: String?): Long {
        if (value == null) return 1L // null is represented by "-"
        val bytes = value.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).size.toLong()
        return (bytes * 4L + 2L) / 3L // URL-safe Base64 without padding
    }

    private fun encodeField(value: String?): String =
        value?.let {
            Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(it.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
        } ?: "-"

    private fun decodeField(value: String): String? {
        if (value == "-") return null
        return String(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(value), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
    }

    private const val MAX_ENCODED_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024
    private const val MAX_QUEUE_ITEMS = 300
    private const val MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH = 16_384
    private const val MAX_PATH_LENGTH = 4_096
    private const val MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 512
    private const val MAX_TORRENT_BYTES = TorrentQueueStore.MAX_TORRENT_BYTES
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/TorrentQueueCodec.kt


========================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/TorrentQueueStore.kt
========================================================================================

package app.jacket.mobile.torrent

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import java.io.File
import java.security.MessageDigest

/** Durable task metadata; native JNI handles deliberately never enter this model. */
data class QueuedTorrent(
    val id: String,
    val title: String,
    val magnetUri: String? = null,
    val torrentPath: String? = null,
    val selectedFileIndex: Int? = null
) {
    init {
        require(magnetUri?.startsWith("magnet:?") == true || !torrentPath.isNullOrBlank()) {
            "Задача должна содержать magnet или сохранённый .torrent"
        }
    }
}

class TorrentQueueStore(context: Context) {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val preferences = appContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFERENCES, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    @Synchronized
    fun all(): List<QueuedTorrent> {
        val raw = preferences.getString(KEY_QUEUE, "[]").orEmpty()
        val isVersion2 = raw.startsWith("JACKET_QUEUE_V2")
        val decoded = if (isVersion2) {
            runCatching { TorrentQueueCodec.decode(raw) }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        } else {
            // Legacy IDs are untrusted: canonicalize magnets and validate file IDs
            // before writing V2, so one malformed record cannot poison the queue.
            runCatching { TorrentQueueCodec.decodeLegacy(raw) }.getOrDefault(emptyList())
        }
        val valid = decoded.filter { it.magnetUri?.startsWith("magnet:?") == true ||
            (!it.torrentPath.isNullOrBlank() && File(it.torrentPath).isFile) }.take(MAX_QUEUE_SIZE)
        if (raw != TorrentQueueCodec.encode(valid)) save(valid)
        return valid
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun get(id: String): QueuedTorrent? = all().firstOrNull { it.id.equals(id, true) }

    @Synchronized
    fun add(magnet: String, title: String): QueuedTorrent {
        require(magnet.startsWith("magnet:?") && magnet.length <= MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH) {
            "Некорректная или слишком длинная magnet-ссылка"
        }
        val item = QueuedTorrent(
            id = idFor(magnet),
            title = title.trim().take(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH).ifBlank { "Торрент" },
            magnetUri = magnet
        )
        upsert(item)
        return item
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun addTorrentFile(torrentFile: File, id: String, title: String): QueuedTorrent {
        require(torrentFile.isFile && torrentFile.length() in 1..MAX_TORRENT_BYTES) {
            "Файл .torrent отсутствует или превышает допустимый размер"
        }
        require(id.matches(INFO_HASH)) { "Некорректный infohash" }
        val item = QueuedTorrent(
            id = id.lowercase(),
            title = title.trim().take(MAX_TITLE_LENGTH).ifBlank { torrentFile.nameWithoutExtension },
            torrentPath = torrentFile.absolutePath
        )
        upsert(item)
        return item
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun selectFile(id: String, index: Int?) {
        val item = get(id) ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Загрузка не найдена")
        require(index == null || index >= 0) { "Индекс файла не может быть отрицательным" }
        upsert(item.copy(selectedFileIndex = index))
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun remove(id: String) {
        save(all().filterNot { it.id.equals(id, true) })
        val paused = preferences.getStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
        paused.remove(id)
        check(preferences.edit().putStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, paused).commit()) {
            "Не удалось надёжно сохранить состояние паузы загрузки"
        }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun setPaused(id: String, paused: Boolean) {
        val values = preferences.getStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
        if (paused) values.add(id) else values.remove(id)
        check(preferences.edit().putStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, values).commit()) {
            "Не удалось надёжно сохранить состояние паузы загрузки"
        }
    }

    fun isPaused(id: String): Boolean = id in preferences.getStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, emptySet()).orEmpty()
    fun pausedIds(): Set<String> = preferences.getStringSet(KEY_PAUSED, emptySet()).orEmpty().toSet()

    @Synchronized
    private fun upsert(item: QueuedTorrent) {
        save((all().filterNot { it.id.equals(item.id, true) } + item).takeLast(MAX_QUEUE_SIZE))
    }

    private fun save(items: List<QueuedTorrent>) {
        // Queue writes happen on the torrent service IO dispatcher. Commit before
        // libtorrent is mutated so a process kill cannot lose the newest magnet,
        // .torrent path or selected-file decision from an asynchronously applied edit.
        check(preferences.edit().putString(KEY_QUEUE, TorrentQueueCodec.encode(items)).commit()) {
            "Не удалось надёжно сохранить очередь торрентов"
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val PREFERENCES = "jacket_torrent_queue"
        private const val KEY_QUEUE = "queue_json"
        private const val KEY_PAUSED = "paused_ids"
        private const val MAX_QUEUE_SIZE = 300
        private const val MAX_MAGNET_LENGTH = 16_384
        private const val MAX_TITLE_LENGTH = 512
        const val MAX_TORRENT_BYTES = 8L * 1024L * 1024L
        private val INFO_HASH = Regex("(?i)([a-f0-9]{40}|[a-f0-9]{64})")

        /** Returns a canonical hex info-hash so queue operations match libtorrent's hash IDs. */
        fun idFor(magnet: String): String {
            val xtValues = runCatching { Uri.parse(magnet).getQueryParameters("xt") }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
            val btih = xtValues.firstOrNull { it.startsWith("urn:btih:", ignoreCase = true) }
                ?.substringAfterLast(':')
                ?.let(::normalizeBtih)
            if (btih != null) return btih

            // BEP 52 magnet links use a multihash prefix (0x12 = SHA-256, 0x20 = 32 bytes).
            val btmh = xtValues.firstOrNull { it.startsWith("urn:btmh:", ignoreCase = true) }
                ?.substringAfterLast(':')
                ?.lowercase()
                ?.removePrefix("1220")
                ?.takeIf { it.matches(Regex("[a-f0-9]{64}")) }
            if (btmh != null) return btmh

            return MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
                .digest(magnet.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
                .take(12)
                .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
        }

        fun idForFile(file: File): String {
            require(file.isFile && file.length() in 1..MAX_TORRENT_BYTES) {
                "Файл .torrent отсутствует или превышает допустимый размер"
            }
            return file.nameWithoutExtension.lowercase().takeIf { it.matches(INFO_HASH) }
                ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Не удалось определить infohash из имени .torrent")
        }

        private fun normalizeBtih(value: String): String? {
            val clean = value.trim()
            if (clean.matches(Regex("[a-fA-F0-9]{40}"))) return clean.lowercase()
            if (!clean.matches(Regex("[A-Z2-7a-z]{32}"))) return null

            val alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567"
            var buffer = 0
            var bits = 0
            val bytes = ArrayList<Byte>(20)
            for (character in clean.uppercase()) {
                val digit = alphabet.indexOf(character)
                if (digit < 0) return null
                buffer = (buffer shl 5) or digit
                bits += 5
                if (bits >= 8) {
                    bits -= 8
                    bytes += ((buffer shr bits) and 0xff).toByte()
                }
            }
            if (bytes.size != 20) return null
            return bytes.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it.toInt() and 0xff) }
        }
    }
}

END FILE: app/src/main/java/app/jacket/mobile/torrent/TorrentQueueStore.kt

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun JacketApp(viewModel: MainViewModel) {
    var selectedTab by rememberSaveable { mutableIntStateOf(TAB_HOME) }
    var showSettings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showDownloads by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var playerUrl by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var playerTitle by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var playerProgressKey by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var playerInitialPosition by rememberSaveable { mutableLongStateOf(0L) }
    var playerSubtitles by remember { mutableStateOf(emptyList<app.jacket.mobile.torrent.PlaybackSubtitle>()) }
    var playerHeaders by remember { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, String>()) }
    var isTorrentPlayback by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val screenStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
    val torrentPlayback by TorrentPlaybackBus.active.collectAsState()
    val details by viewModel.selectedDetails.collectAsState()
    val isTv = (LocalConfiguration.current.uiMode and android.content.res.Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_MASK) == android.content.res.Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_TELEVISION
